### No script runs in a frame: the five Range-*Contents/insertNode/surroundContents files drive an iframe's run()

- **4188 subtests / 12 files**, size L, overlaps REALM, confidence high
- Root cause: The realm is shared (lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp header, lines 30-40: 'NO SCRIPTS RUN IN A FRAME'), so Range-test-iframe.html's `run` never exists and restoreIframe() throws '`run` is undefined, not a function'. Range-insertNode/surroundContents report every subtest FAIL instead of HARNESS_ERROR because their loop catches per subtest. The same gap explains the frame-realm identity files in this slice: create-element-realm-after-adoption ('Cannot read properties of undefined (reading set)' - the frame's Document.prototype), TreeWalker cross-realm null-browsing-context, MutationObserver-cross-realm-callback-report-exception, abort-signal-timeout (iframe.contentWindow.AbortSignal is the [..]
- Spec: HTML 8.1.3 realms/settings objects, 7.4 navigables; ECMA-262 9.3 Realm Records, 10.2.3 OrdinaryFunctionCreate step 4, 10.2.1.1 PrepareForOrdinaryCall step 6
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp (load_frame line 218, the contentWindow proxy at ~line 330); ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/** (realm record, closure [[Realm]], context::call switching); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/nested.cpp, scripts.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/abort.cpp:200-220 (AbortSignal.timeout's timer must belong to the frame's event loop)
- Evidence: dom/ranges/Range-insertNode.html 1840/1840 FAIL; dom/ranges/Range-surroundContents.html 1840/1840 FAIL; dom/ranges/Range-extractContents.html 186/187 HARNESS_ERROR '`run` is undefined, not a function - in `restoreIframe`'; dom/ranges/Range-cloneContents.html 186/187 HARNESS_ERROR; dom/ranges/Range-deleteContents.html 124/125 HARNESS_ERROR
- Fix: Round-eight REALM brief as written: a realm = globals table + global object recorded on every closure, context::call switches for the call's duration; frames.cpp makes a realm per frame document whose window IS the realm's global (the proxy over the page's globals goes away) and runs the frame's <script>s under it. For this slice batch (2) of the brief is the payoff: Range-test-iframe.html defines `run` in the frame. abort-signal-timeout additionally needs the frame's timers dropped when the frame is removed (frames_ swap at reconcile_frames: cancel timers whose realm is the departed frame's).

### No server: WPT's python handlers (.py), .headers sidecars and redirect.py are not emulated by the frame loader

- **639 subtests / 5 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: frames.cpp says it: 'THE CONTENT TYPE COMES FROM THE NAME, because there is no server to send one' (mime_for_path, line 107) and load_frame (line 218) hands `assets_->load(src)` the raw bytes. characterset-helper.js sets `iframe.src = "encoding.py?label=" + label`; encoding.py is a 5-line wptserve handler returning `<!doctype html><meta charset="LABEL">`. The registry serves the python SOURCE as application/octet-stream (extension `py` unknown), the frame gets an empty document, and `contentDocument.characterSet` is 'UTF-8' for all 636 labels except the UTF-8 ones. The label table itself is complete (lib/DOM/encoding.cpp:30 has "866"->IBM866, :129 cskoi8r->KOI8-R; encoding_from_label at [..]
- Spec: HTML 13.2.3.2 determining the character encoding (steps 4, 8); Encoding Standard §4.2 get an encoding (labels); Fetch 4.4 HTTP-redirect fetch
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp:107 (mime_for_path) and :218-300 (load_frame: type, bytes, prescan_encoding/set_encoding); ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/shell/page/assets.hpp:26 (asset_registry::load/find); ctbrowser/lib/DOM/encoding.cpp:386 (encoding_from_label), :428 (prescan_encoding); tools/wpt/run-wpt.py (alternative: serve the corpus through a real wptserve)
- Evidence: dom/nodes/Document-characterSet-normalization-1.html 297/315 FAIL 'Name "IBM866" has label "866" (characterSet): expected "IBM866" but got "UTF-8"' (the 18 PASS are the UTF-8 labels); dom/nodes/Document-characterSet-normalization-2.html 339/339 FAIL; dom/nodes/Document-contentType/contentType/contenttype_mimeheader_01.html 1/1 'expected "text/xml" but got "application/octet-stream"'; [..]
- Fix: In load_frame, before the extension lookup: (1) if a `<path>.headers` sidecar exists in the registry, parse its `Content-Type:` line - the MIME becomes `type`, and a `charset=` parameter becomes `fresh.set_encoding(encoding_from_label(param))` ahead of the prescan (HTML 13.2.3.2 step 4: a transport-layer encoding wins over <meta>); (2) a tiny table of the WPT handlers the corpus reaches, keyed by path suffix: `dom/nodes/encoding.py?label=X` -> bytes `<!doctype html><meta charset="X">` as text/html (prescan_encoding then maps the label; assert the canonical name comes out of encoding_from_label so `characterSet` reads IBM866), `/common/redirect.py?location=Y` -> load Y and observe_location(Y) so contentDocument.URL is the target. Keep the table beside mime_for_path with the same 'small on purpose' note. The alternative - run-wpt.py starting wptserve - is bigger and changes every [..]

### JS strings are UTF-8 bytes: every test that slices or measures a non-ASCII string disagrees with the DOM's UTF-16 offsets

- **277 subtests / 7 files**, size L, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: docs/script.md 'The UTF-16 gap' (line 1027): a JS string is stored as UTF-8 bytes, so `.length`, `substring`, `[i]` count bytes. The Range-mutations data files use paras[0] = 'Äb̈...' and compute the expected data in JS (`data.substr(0, offset) + ...`) with byte offsets while replaceData/deleteData (character_data.cpp) edit in UTF-16 units - every 'Sanity check failed: data not changed as expected' is this (the two strings print identically but differ in where the combining mark landed). Range-selectNode's 'endOffset must equal node length (25) expected 25 but got 17' is `node.data.length` (25 bytes) against the DOM's 17 units; Node-properties' detachedForeignComment.length 'expected 19 [..]
- Spec: ECMA-262 6.1.4 The String Type (UTF-16 code units); DOM 4.10 CharacterData 'length' in code units
- Engine files: ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/script/value.hpp (string_object representation); ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/string*.cpp, regexp (every index-taking method); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/character_data.cpp (the DOM boundary: units_length / offset conversion); ctbrowser/unittests/js/string_basics.cpp (pins the current wrong answers with V8's beside them - the acceptance list)
- Evidence: dom/ranges/Range-mutations-replaceData.html 126 'Sanity check failed' of 256 failing; dom/ranges/Range-mutations-deleteData.html 58 of 108; dom/ranges/Range-mutations-insertData.html 24 of 52; dom/ranges/Range-mutations-appendData.html 38/38; dom/nodes/getElementsByClassName-whitespace-class-names.html 25/26 'expected 1 but got 3'
- Fix: Not a brief, a plan (wpt-next.md says so): change string_object to UTF-16 code units (or a Latin-1/UTF-16 dual representation as V8/SpiderMonkey do), then every builtin that takes an index (charAt/charCodeAt/codePointAt/slice/substring/indexOf/split/normalize/the regex engine's cursor) and the DOM boundary (cx.string(std::string) <-> UTF-8 for atoms and attribute values, character_data.cpp's offsets become identity). string_basics.cpp's labelled section is the acceptance list. Nothing smaller helps this slice: the tests compute the expectation in JS, so the DOM side is already right.

### NodeIterator pre-removing steps are not implemented

- **28 subtests / 3 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: traversal.cpp:377-380 says it in a ponytail note: 'the pre-removing steps ... need a hook where the tree is edited, which this file cannot reach'. An iterator's referenceNode slot keeps pointing at the removed node. The other 21 subtests of NodeIterator-removal.html ('parameter 1 is not of type Node') are a cascade: the first two asserts throw before the test's `oldParent.insertBefore(node, oldSibling)` restore, paras[0] stays detached and `paras[0].firstChild` is null for every later root loop. The hook now exists: since bd75b1b7 the document's write log carries `removed` notes with (parent, child, index) and mutated() (entry.cpp:297) already runs settle_live_ranges over them.
- Spec: DOM 6.1 NodeIterator pre-removing steps; 4.2.3 remove step 4 ('for each NodeIterator object iterator whose root's node document is node's node document, run the NodeIterator pre-removing steps'); 6.1 [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/traversal.cpp:377-445 (install_iterator; reference_slot/before_slot); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/entry.cpp:285-300 (mutated: add settle_node_iterators(writes) beside settle_live_ranges) and :136 (mark the registered iterators); ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/shell/bindings.hpp:2542-2565 (live_ranges_ pattern: add node_iterators_); [..]
- Evidence: dom/traversal/NodeIterator-removal.html 25/25 ('.referenceNode of document.createNodeIterator(document) advanced 14 times expected <div id=test> but got <p id=a>', then 21x the cascade); dom/traversal/NodeIterator-removal-during-filtering.html 2/4 'referenceNode retargeted to a expected <a-el> but got <b-el>'; dom/nodes/moveBefore/moveBefore-nodeiterator.html 1/1 'moveBefore() runs NodeIterator [..]
- Fix: Register every NodeIterator object in a node_iterators_ vector (as live_ranges_ is, marked from entry.cpp:136). In mutated(), for each `removed` note (parent P, child C, index i) and each iterator whose root is not inside C and whose referenceNode is C or a descendant: if pointerBeforeReferenceNode is true, set reference to the node following C's last inclusive descendant within root - computable AFTER the removal as P.children[i] if it exists (then its first... no: the node itself), else the next following node of P within root; if none, set reference to the node preceding C (P.children[i-1]'s last inclusive descendant, or P) and pointerBeforeReferenceNode false. If pointerBeforeReferenceNode is false, set reference to the preceding node (same computation). moveBefore takes the same path (insert_node logs removed+inserted). removal-during-filtering additionally needs the in-flight [..]

### Namespace selector `|div` / `|*` (no namespace) is compiled as dead

- **16 subtests / 1 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: lib/Style/css/selector.cpp:868-876: for ns_prefix::none the compiler sets `dead = true` with the comment 'The null namespace is answered by nothing in the matcher, which knows an element's namespace only as html, svg or other'. That is no longer true: document::element_namespace (document.hpp:108/245, 'the empty string is the null namespace') records an `other` element's URI, and ParentNode-querySelector-All.js:39 makes `no-namespace-div3` with createElementNS(null, 'div'). engine.cpp:1061-1066 matches only a named ns_uri (html/svg URIs).
- Spec: Selectors 4 §5.2 type selectors and namespaces ('|E' = elements with no namespace); DOM 4.9 createElementNS
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/selector.cpp:868-876 (read_namespaced: ns_prefix::none -> dead); ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp:1055-1066 (element namespace check) and :1113-1114 (the attribute form already handles `none`); ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/style/selector.hpp:116/229 (compound ns field)
- Evidence: dom/nodes/ParentNode-querySelector-All.html 16/1975 FAIL: 'Namespace selector, matching div elements in no namespace only: #no-namespace |div ... expected 1 but got 0' x4 contexts x {querySelector, querySelectorAll} x {|div, |*} - the only failures in the file, so it flips to PASS
- Fix: Keep the compound's ns as ns_prefix::none instead of marking it dead; in engine.cpp beside the ns_uri check: `if (c.ns == ns_prefix::none && !(ns == node_ns::other && txn.element_namespace(node) == atom{})) return false;` (an HTML or SVG element always has a namespace). One unit test in unittests/unit for createElementNS(null,'x') matched by `|x` and not by `x` under a default @namespace.

### HTMLCollection is not a legacy platform object: expandos shadow indices/names, delete succeeds, indices past 2^32-2 count as indices, `length` has no brand check

- **10 subtests / 4 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: collections.cpp's handler has `get`, `set`, `has`, `getOwnPropertyDescriptor`, `ownKeys` only - no `defineProperty` and no `deleteProperty` trap, so Object.defineProperty(coll, idx/name, ...) lands on the target and `delete coll[0]` returns true; the `set` trap (:595-621) refuses `length` and indices but stores any other key as an expando even when it is a supported property name (WebIDL 3.9.3 step 2.2.1 says return false when the name is supported and there is no named setter); `whole_index` (:144-152) has no upper bound, so "4294967295".."4294967297" are indices (WebIDL: an array index is < 2^32-1) and the element with that id is never found by name; the `length` accessor (:272-279) asks [..]
- Spec: WebIDL 3.9.3 [[DefineOwnProperty]], 3.9.4 [[Delete]], 3.9.1 [[GetOwnProperty]] (array index property name < 2^32-1), 3.7.6 attribute getters' brand check
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/collections.cpp:144 (whole_index), :272 (length accessor), :578-661 (traps)
- Evidence: dom/collections/HTMLCollection-supported-property-indices.html 4/7 ('Handling of property names that look like integers around 2^32: expected <foo id=-2> but got null', 'Trying to set an expando that would shadow an already-existing indexed property', 'Trying to set an expando with an indexed property name past the end of the list: did not throw', 'Trying to delete an indexed property name should [..]
- Fix: (1) whole_index: return nullopt when at >= 4294967295. (2) Add `defineProperty` and `deleteProperty` traps: an index or a supported name (held->names, or a name that is currently exposed) -> return false (strict callers throw TypeError; Object.defineProperty throws) ; delete of an unsupported key -> ordinary. (3) `set` trap: same refusal for supported names, so `coll.named = x` does not create the expando (and the 'gets added later' case: refresh() must not skip a key because an expando exists - the expando should never have been created). (4) `length`/`item`/`namedItem`: read the hidden state key as an OWN property of `this` (c.own_property, not lookup_property) and throw TypeError 'Illegal invocation' otherwise. Requires the VM to honour a false return from deleteProperty in strict code - check vm/objects/store.cpp's delete path; the file's comment 'this engine has no strict mode' may [..]

### Focus fixup on moveBefore into an inert/hidden subtree is not modelled

- **8 subtests / 3 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence medium
- Root cause: browser/scripts.cpp:65-72 runs HTML's focus fixup rule only for 'a focused element that left the document' (`!is_connected(focused_) || focus_was_moved()`). moveBefore keeps the element connected while moving it under `inert`, `hidden`, or `display:none`, which makes it no longer a focusable area; the rule must then move focus to the body at the next rendering update, firing focusout (and :focus-within must be recomputed along the new ancestor chain).
- Spec: HTML 6.6.1 focusable area, 6.6.2 'focus fixup rule' (run during update the rendering); HTML 6.7 inert subtrees; DOM 4.2.3 move
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/scripts.cpp:60-75 (the fixup hook); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/frame.cpp (the rendering-update step where 'focusable area' should be re-evaluated); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/node_methods.cpp:327-380 (moveBefore)
- Evidence: dom/nodes/moveBefore/focus-preserve.html 3/5 'Focus is fixed up after a rendering update expected <body> but got <button>' (into inert subtree, into hidden parent, ancestor moved); dom/nodes/moveBefore/focus-within.html 3/5 ':focus-within should be eventually up to date when moving to an inert subtree' (chain lists old_parent); dom/nodes/moveBefore/fire-focusin-focusout.html 2/5 'expected [..]
- Fix: At the rendering update (frame.cpp, after style): if focused_ is set and the element is no longer a focusable area - an inclusive ancestor has `inert` or `hidden`, or its computed display is none - run the fixup: fire focusout/blur at it, clear state_focus, set focused_ = body (the viewport's focused area). :focus-within is derived from the focused chain, so recompute it in the same step (set_state on the old and new ancestor chains). moveBefore itself needs nothing: DOM 'move' keeps focus, and the fixup is what the spec relies on.

### Range boundaries across a shadow boundary are refused ('the node is in another tree')

- **8 subtests / 3 files**, size M, overlaps FLAT, confidence medium
- Root cause: range.cpp's set_boundary (~line 330) collapses the other point when `root_of(node) != root_of(other)` - root_of walks parent_of, which stops at a shadow root (a shadow root has no parent in the node tree). Selection.setBaseAndExtent(p.firstChild, 3, shadowRoot.firstChild.firstChild, 5) then produces a collapsed range and the *-around-shadow.tentative files expect the pre-flat-tree Range algorithms over a composed selection (a Range whose ends are in different trees, cloned/extracted 'as in the DOM rather than the flat tree').
- Spec: Selection API (tentative) composed live ranges; DOM 5.5 Range algorithms; whatwg/dom#1342
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/range.cpp (set_boundary, root_of, compare_order, extract_from); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/selection.cpp (setBaseAndExtent)
- Evidence: dom/ranges/Range-extractContents-around-shadow.tentative.html 3/7 'the node is in another tree'; dom/ranges/Range-cloneContents-around-shadow.tentative.html 3/5; dom/ranges/Range-deleteContents-around-shadow.tentative.html 2/2
- Fix: Tentative spec (Selection API 'composed live range'): the Selection's range is allowed to have boundary points in different trees when both are in the same composed tree; the Range algorithms then see the boundary in the shadow root as if the host were the container (for cloneContents/extractContents the host's light children are what is cloned; deleteContents removes up to the host). Low priority - tentative and only Chromium implements it; do after FLAT.

### A <template>'s content fragment lives in the template's own document, not an inert 'template contents owner document'

- **3 subtests / 2 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: wrapper.cpp:88-104: template.content is `doc_->template_content(id)` or a fragment from `doc_->create_fragment()` - a fragment of the SAME document, so `df.ownerDocument === document` and adoptNode(df) is a no-op. HTML 4.12.3 gives every document an associated inert template contents owner document, and the fragment's host is the template; adoption of the template must move the content to the new document's contents owner document.
- Spec: HTML 4.12.3 'appropriate template contents owner document', template's adopting steps; DOM 4.2.4 adopt
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/wrapper.cpp:88-104; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/second_document.cpp (the model for a second dom_bindings over its own tree); ctbrowser/lib/DOM/document.cpp (template_content storage)
- Evidence: dom/nodes/adoption.window.js 2/6 'appendChild() and DocumentFragment with host: assert_not_equals: got disallowed value Document node with 2 children' (df.ownerDocument !== document expected); dom/nodes/Document-adoptNode-DocumentFragment-with-host.window.js 1/7 'adoptNode() <template> element: content follows via template adopting steps'
- Fix: Give the primary bindings one lazily-made secondary document (adopt_second_document, browsing context null, 'inert') as the template contents owner; template.content creates its fragment there with host = the template element; the template's adopting steps (adoptNode/appendChild across documents, DOM 4.2.4 step 3.1.4) move the content fragment to the target document's owner document. The `host` back-reference is what the moveBefore/'DocumentFragment with host' rows read.

### Collection index reads are a full proxy-trap call each: the six NodeList-static-length-getter-tampered files never finish

- **0 subtests / 6 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence medium
- Root cause: querySelectorAll's static NodeList (node_methods.cpp:415-429) and every live collection are script::proxy_objects whose `get` trap (collections.cpp:578-591) runs per read: number->string key conversion by the VM, `refresh(c)` (a doc_->version() read), `whole_index` (from_chars), then `member`. `length` is an accessor that calls the hidden state native through cx.call (collections.cpp:272-279). NodeList-static-length-tampered.js reads `nodeList[j]` 5e6 times per call and the test calls it 50x (250M reads); the native-call safepoint in run_loop.cpp:642-656 (already in the baseline) stops the allocation ceiling but not the cost, so all six files run past the runner's 60 s (m JSON: 1 CRASH [..]
- Spec: WebIDL 3.9 legacy platform objects [[Get]] (indexed property getter); ECMA-262 10.5 Proxy [[Get]] is what the engine literally does today
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/collections.cpp:402-661 (make_live_collection: the target/handler proxy, get trap :578, length accessor :272); ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/objects/lookup.cpp:97 and :208 (proxy get path; where a numeric key becomes a string); ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/script/vm.hpp:396-420 (allocation ceiling)
- Evidence: dom/nodes/NodeList-static-length-getter-tampered-1.html CRASH 'driver died mid-command: SIGABRT' (m JSON) / TIMEOUT (w JSON); dom/nodes/NodeList-static-length-getter-tampered-{2,3,indexOf-1,indexOf-2,indexOf-3}.html TIMEOUT 'the page never yielded'; docs/wpt.md line ~325: 'NodeList-static-length-getter-tampered-* then hit the VM's 40,000,000-object allocation ceiling - a NodeList index read [..]
- Fix: Give the VM an indexed fast path for these objects instead of a JS-visible handler: a `collection_object` heap kind (or an optional C++ `index_hook` on object_object) that lookup_index/lookup_property consult for integer keys before the generic proxy path - no key stringification, no native frame, no args vector; the hook reads held->members[i] after the version check and returns the cached wrapper (`value_of_wrapper`). `length` likewise through the hook. Keep the proxy handler for named keys, ownKeys and descriptors. Measure with the tampered-1 file alone (it was 1.1-2.0 s to first result before the ceiling hit); the target is 250M reads in under 60 s, i.e. <240 ns per read.

**Long tail:** 42 subtests in 27 moveBefore files beyond the focus/NodeIterator/live-range/iframe rows are missing features, not moveBefore: 8 TIMEOUT continue-css-transition-*/css-transition-* (wait for transition events that a move resets or never fire), popover-preserve/modal-dialog (showPopover/showModal undefined), slotchange-events 4 + moveBefore-shadow-inside/from-light-to-shadow/custom-element-move-reactions (FLAT), nonce (nonce attribute hiding), stylesheet-no-refetch, select-option-optgroup (option selectedness on move), moveBefore-size-query (container queries), child-style-preserve, [..]

### No realm per browsing context: a frame's scripts never run, its window is a proxy over the page's globals, errors are reported to the page's window

- **158 subtests / 76 files**, size L, overlaps REALM, confidence high
- Root cause: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp header still says it at HEAD ('THE REALM IS SHARED', 'NO SCRIPTS RUN IN A FRAME'); `rg run_script_ lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp` finds nothing. load_frame() builds contentWindow as a small object_object behind a proxy whose miss reads cx.global() (frames.cpp:340-372), so frame.contentWindow.Function === Function, `frames[0].onerror`/`frames[0].event` are plain props on that object, and every helper an iframe's <script> defines (`run`, `doParse`, `callDocumentMethod`, `runWindowOpenVeryIndirectly`, `frameState`, `listener`, `hey`) is undefined (17 subtests carry exactly that message). dispatch_error_value (events/input.cpp:85) always fires at the PAGE [..]

- Fix: Per round8-brief-REALM.md. For this slice the observable deliverables are: (1) run a frame's inline and src <script>s and inline handlers under the frame's realm at load_frame/settle_frame time; (2) closure_object records its realm; context::call switches globals for the call; (3) dispatch_error_value takes the realm of the throwing callback (listener/timer/rAF/microtask) and fires `error` at THAT realm's window object, with `window.event` set on that window only; (4) document.open()/write in a frame runs against the frame's bindings and realm (already routed via `[this]` in install_dynamic_markup; only the scripts are missing). ### structuredClone cannot serialize platform objects and has no transfer list

- **48 subtests / 1 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: The cloner in ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/text/globals.cpp:150-410 says it: 'Platform objects (Blob, File, ImageData) are the host's: this engine cannot tell one from a plain object and copies it as one', so `structuredClone(new Blob())` returns a plain object (23 'instanceof Blob/File/FileList expected true got false'). There is no options.transfer (`rg transfer globals.cpp` finds nothing) so 'Resizable ArrayBuffer is transferable' etc. see byteLength 16 not 0 and detached-buffer transfer never rejects (6). A detached/OOB DataView throws the VM's RangeError instead of DataCloneError (2). MessagePort/ImageBitmap/OffscreenCanvas/ReadableStream/Response do not exist (8). 'A subclass [..]
- Spec: HTML 2.7.3 StructuredSerializeInternal (platform objects with [Serializable]), 2.7.6 StructuredSerializeWithTransfer / 2.7.7 StructuredDeserializeWithTransfer, [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/text/globals.cpp (cloner, install_structured_clone); ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/script/vm.hpp (a host serialize/deserialize hook beside set_rejection_tracker); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/window/window.cpp (Blob/File/FileList/ImageData/DOMException as [Serializable]); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/exceptions.cpp
- Evidence: html/webappapis/structured-clone/structured-clone.any.js (48 of 152: 23 Blob/File/FileList instanceof, 11 createImageBitmap/OffscreenCanvas/MessageChannel/ReadableStream/Response not defined, 6 transfer, 2 DataCloneError type, 1 BigInt, 5 misc)
- Fix: Add context::set_platform_serializer(std::function<bool(value, serialized&)>, deserializer) that the cloner consults for any object with a host brand before falling to the plain-object walk; lib/Shell registers Blob (bytes+type), File (+name,lastModified), FileList, ImageData, DOMException, DOMPoint/DOMRect/DOMMatrix, keyed by the prototype chain's nearest registered interface (gives the subclass and deleted-global cases for free). Parse `options.transfer` in structuredClone: ArrayBuffer -> new buffer, source detached (byteLength 0, later access throws TypeError); throw DataCloneError (via the existing throw_data_clone_error) for a detached or non-transferable value, and map the VM's OOB DataView RangeError to DataCloneError inside the cloner. MessagePort/ImageBitmap/OffscreenCanvas stay long tail.

- Root cause: set_html_unsafe (ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/shadow_dom.cpp:324-350) takes only the markup; the binding at :505 passes arg 0 and never reads args[1]. The fragment is parsed into a scratch document with `doc_->scripting()` and copied, so a <script> in it is inert even with {runScripts:true} (18 subtests: 'assert_true: expected true got false' + TIMEOUT/NOTRUN waiting for the external script's load; ordering vs custom element reactions 2). For a <template> target the children go under the template element instead of its content fragment (1: 'template should not have any child nodes'). In an XML/SVG document `parse_html_fragment(..., txn.element_ns(target))` yields '' because the context [..]
- Spec: HTML 8.6.2 setHTMLUnsafe(html, options) and 'run scripts' steps (whatwg/html#12495), 4.12.1.1 prepare the script element, 4.12.1.2 execute, 8.6 'template' target
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/shadow_dom.cpp (set_html_unsafe, install_shadow_dom setHTMLUnsafe binding); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/entry.cpp (run_inserted_scripts / execute_script_element - reuse for 'run scripts' in tree order); ctbrowser/lib/DOM/treebuilder.cpp (fragment parsing with an XML context element)

- Fix: Read `SetHTMLUnsafeOptions {runScripts}` from args[1]. Parse the fragment with the scripting flag = runScripts (so <noscript> etc. behave); after the copy, if runScripts: collect every <script> in the inserted subtree in tree order, mark them 'already started' cleared, then run prepare-the-script-element for each (inline: execute now with currentScript rules; src: fetch via assets_ and run in a later task with load event) - the existing execute_script_element does the run; do it BEFORE custom element reactions are flushed (test expects 'run-scripts' before 'element-1-ctor'). Template target: if target is <template>, the copy destination is its content fragment's node (the same as innerHTML on a template). ### Only one activation target must run: <area href> never follows its link, and a nested form's submit reaches the outer form's onsubmit

- **18 subtests / 1 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence medium
- Root cause: browser::follow_link (ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/actions.cpp:132-145) walks ancestors looking for `atoms_.intern_lower("a")` only, so activate() for an <area> (actions.cpp:23 'tag == a || tag == area' -> follow_link) finds no href and no hashchange fires: all 10 '<AREA> child of ...' cases get [] instead of the '#testN_link' URL. The 8 nested-form cases (an inner <form> appended inside the outer form's <input type=submit>/<button>) record BOTH forms: the engine dispatches `submit` at the inner form with bubbles=true and the outer form's onsubmit content attribute runs with `this` = outer form; browsers record only the inner one (unverified whether they fire submit non-bubbling across a [..]
- Spec: HTML 4.8.14 the area element (activation behaviour = follow the hyperlink), DOM 2.9 dispatch steps 5.5-5.9.11 (one activation target), HTML 4.10.21.3 form submission algorithm
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/actions.cpp (follow_link: accept `area`; submit/activate); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/dispatch.cpp (has_activation_behavior / run_activation_behavior)
- Evidence: dom/events/Event-dispatch-single-activation-behavior.html (18 of 132: 10 AREA cases 'expected ["...#testNN_link"] got []', 8 nested FORM submit/reset cases 'expected length 1 got 2')
- Fix: follow_link: match `a` OR `area` (HTML 4.8.14: an <area> with href is a hyperlink with the same activation behaviour); the fragment branch then fires hashchange as today. For the form cases, measure what happens with `<form onsubmit="log(this)"><input type=submit><form onsubmit=log(this)><input type=submit id=x></form></input></form>` in Chrome; likely fix is in browser::submit: the form submission algorithm's 'submit' event must not be observed by an outer form - i.e. the engine must not treat the outer form as a second activation - rather than a change to bubbling.

Steps 7-8 of the open steps erase [..]
- Spec: HTML 8.4.2 document open steps (steps 7-8 remove listeners on document, nodes and window; step 9 replace all; steps 13-16 new parser), 13.2.6.4.1 'initial' insertion mode
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/write.cpp (document_open); ctbrowser/lib/DOM/treebuilder.cpp (begin/pump for an open stream on an empty document; doctype insertion at :999-1014); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/dispatch.cpp (which bindings own a listener added on a secondary document's nodes); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp (frame window focus())

- Fix: (a) In tree_builder::begin for open=true with no eager root, make sure `start()` resets root_/insertion-mode to 'initial' so the first written doctype/element becomes a child of the Document (unit test: createHTMLDocument().open(); write('<!DOCTYPE html>'); childNodes.length==1; close(); ==2). (b) document_open must erase listeners for this document's nodes/window/document wherever they are stored: erase_if over primary().listeners_ (and this->listeners_) by owner-of-node == this, plus this document's window bucket. ### Setting the details `open` attribute must queue the toggle task (the WPT queueTask() idiom)

- **13 subtests / 2 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: `toggle` is dispatched only from browser::toggle_details (ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/actions.cpp:97) on a click. HTML 4.11.1 says the details toggle event task is queued whenever the `open` attribute is added/removed. support/promise-rejection-events.js:885-891 implements queueTask(f) as `d = createElement('details'); d.ontoggle = f; d.setAttribute('open','')` on a DISCONNECTED element, so at HEAD - where round seven's unhandledrejection landed - the 7+ subtests that use queueTask still never resume.
- Spec: HTML 4.11.1 the details element ('queue a details toggle event task' whenever the open attribute is added or removed; details toggle task tracker)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/dispatch.cpp (settle_attribute_writes: add an `open` attribute-change hook); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/actions.cpp (toggle_details: share the task); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/entry.cpp (mutated(): attribute write notes)
- Evidence: html/webappapis/scripting/processing-model-2/unhandled-promise-rejections/promise-rejection-events.html (7 subtests naming queueTask: 'delayed handling: a nested-queueTask ...' x3, 'mutationObserverMicrotask vs. queueTask', 'queueTask ordering ... (1)' and '(2)', 'a nested-task delay' - all TIMEOUT at baseline, and gated on this at HEAD); [..]
- Fix: In settle_attribute_writes (or a new `details` hook in mutated()): when a write note is an attribute edit of `open` on an HTML <details>, if no toggle task is pending for that element, queue a task (add_timer 0 / the task queue) that fires a non-bubbling ToggleEvent {oldState, newState} at the element - connected or not. toggle_details then just sets/removes the attribute and lets the hook fire. Unit test: disconnected details, ontoggle, setAttribute('open') -> fires next tick, exactly once for two attribute flips.

MessageChannel = two MessagePort EventTargets; port.postMessage queues the same task to the other port; port.start()/close(); onmessage setter enables. ~150 lines in a new bindings/messaging.cpp. Also unblocks the WPT harness's cross-frame paths and 10+ files outside this slice (html/browsers, dom/events).

### A document.write()n external classic script runs inside write() instead of after the writing script returns

- **5 subtests / 5 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: prepare_parser_script (ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/write.cpp:180-215) fetches an external non-defer script synchronously and runs it immediately (`run_script_(prepared)`) even when the parser's script nesting level is > 0 - i.e. from inside document.write() called by the outer script. HTML: an external classic script is the 'pending parsing-blocking script'; 13.2.6.4.8 sets the parser pause flag when nesting != 0 and the script runs only once the outer script's `</script>` handling unwinds. Observed order [1,3,2,4] vs [1,2,3,4] (script_005/006), and the written <meta> after the script is tokenised too early (script_010/011/012: 'expected 0 but got 1/2').
- Spec: HTML 4.12.1.1 prepare the script element step 33 ('pending parsing-blocking script'), 13.2.6.4.8 the 'text' insertion mode, </script> end tag steps 12-20, 13.2.5 parser pause flag
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/write.cpp (prepare_parser_script step 31/33); ctbrowser/lib/DOM/treebuilder.cpp (script nesting level, parser pause flag, 13.2.6.4.8 steps 13-20)
- Evidence: html/webappapis/dynamic-markup-insertion/document-write/script_005.html (1); html/webappapis/dynamic-markup-insertion/document-write/script_006.html (1); html/webappapis/dynamic-markup-insertion/document-write/script_010.html (1); html/webappapis/dynamic-markup-insertion/document-write/script_011.html (1); html/webappapis/dynamic-markup-insertion/document-write/script_012.html (1)
- Fix: When `parser_->script_nesting() > 0` and the prepared script is external+classic, store it as `pending_parsing_blocking_script_` and set the tree builder's pause flag (stop pumping); in the `</script>` end-tag step, after the outer script returns and nesting drops to 0, run the pending script (and any it writes, in a loop per 13.2.6.4.8 step 20), then resume the pump. Inline written scripts keep running immediately (that path is right today).

So `window.dispatchEvent(new Event('error'))` hands the handler 5 args ('expected 1 but got 5', window-synthetic-event, body-element-synthetic-event, frameset-element-synthetic-event) and a frame window (a different object) never gets the special path (eventhandler-cancellation: 'Returning true from frames[0]'s onerror ... [..]

- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/dispatch.cpp (fire_handler_property; forwards_to_window/body_or_frameset_of); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/interfaces.cpp (error_event_prototype_ for the instanceof check)
