### The event loop runs all due timers / all rAF callbacks then ONE microtask checkpoint, and fires DOMContentLoaded/load ahead of tasks queued earlier in the tick

- **3 subtests / 3 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: run_due_callbacks (ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/window/callbacks.cpp:115-215) loops every due timer calling call_fenced, then drains microtasks once; same for animation frames. HTML's 'clean up after running a callback' performs a checkpoint after EACH callback: promise-resolution-order expects raf1,resolve1,raf2,resolve2 and gets raf1,raf2,resolve1,resolve2. browser::tick (browser/frame.cpp:107-119) fires DOMContentLoaded/complete/load BEFORE advance_clock/run_due_callbacks, so the 'notify about rejected promises' task queued during parsing (promise_rejections.cpp uses add_timer 0 as its task queue) runs after readystatechange:complete instead of between interactive and complete. There is [..]
- Spec: HTML 8.1.7.1 task queues / 8.1.7.3 event loop processing model step 3-7 (one task, then a microtask checkpoint), 8.1.3.4 clean up after running a callback, 8.6 timer initialisation steps (nesting [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/window/callbacks.cpp (run_due_callbacks); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/frame.cpp (tick: load-event ordering vs queued tasks); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/promise_rejections.cpp (its task lives on the timer list); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/write.cpp (parser_finished's 'the end' task); ctbrowser/lib/App/app/run.cpp (fixed_dt / next_wakeup granularity for the [..]
- Evidence: html/webappapis/scripting/processing-model-2/unhandled-promise-rejections/promise-resolution-order.html (1: got raf1,raf2,resolve1,resolve2,...); html/webappapis/scripting/processing-model-2/unhandled-promise-rejections/promise-rejection-event-during-parse.html (1: unhandledrejection after readystatechange:complete); html/webappapis/timers/timer-nesting-not-inherited-in-microtask.html (1: [..]
- Fix: Introduce a real task queue in dom_bindings: `std::deque<task>` with a source tag; setTimeout/rAF/frame-load/fetch/'the end'/rejection notification all become tasks (timers stay a due-list that ENQUEUES a task when due). run_due_callbacks pops one task, runs it, drains microtasks, repeat until the queue is empty for this tick; rAF callbacks each get a checkpoint. browser::tick: the DOMContentLoaded/load task must be queued through the same queue behind whatever the parser's scripts queued (parser_finished already does this for a script-created parser - make `initial` do the same instead of load_event_pending_). For the headless driver, let next_wakeup_ms honour sub-frame timers (a 1ms timer wakes in 1ms; run.cpp already computes `due`). Ordering-sensitive: run dom/events + html/webappapis + the unit suite after each step.

### Timer arguments are not WebIDL-converted: long overflow and non-callable handlers

- **3 subtests / 3 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: install_timers (ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/window/callbacks.cpp:301-306) passes `arg_number(args,1)` straight into add_timer: a delay of 2**32 stays 4294967296 ms instead of wrapping to 0 through `long` (ToInt32) as WebIDL requires, so `setTimeout(done, 2**32)` never fires and `assert_unreached` at 100ms does. A handler that is an object (evil-spec-example) is neither a string nor callable, so run_due_callbacks calls call_fenced on a non-callable: HTML says a non-callable handler is ToString'd at INVOCATION time and run as a script (its toString may itself call setTimeout).
- Spec: HTML 8.6 timer initialisation steps (step 2 'if timeout < 0 set 0', handler is a string -> classic script at run time), WebIDL 3.2.7 long conversion, IDL `setTimeout(TimerHandler handler, optional [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/window/callbacks.cpp (install_timers, run_due_callbacks, add_timer)
- Evidence: html/webappapis/timers/type-long-settimeout.any.js (1); html/webappapis/timers/type-long-setinterval.any.js (1); html/webappapis/timers/evil-spec-example.any.js (1 TIMEOUT)
- Fix: delay = ToInt32(to_number(arg1)) (modulo 2^32 into signed), then max(0,·); keep the callback VALUE (any) and at fire time: callable -> call; otherwise ToString then compile+run as a classic script (the string path already exists - move the coercion to the run site). Also pass extra args (setTimeout(f, 0, a, b)) which run_due_callbacks drops today.

### No History interface on window (history is not defined)

- **2 subtests / 4 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: `rg pushState|"history"|history_ lib include` finds nothing except frames.cpp's exclusion list: window.history does not exist on the page or on a frame window. Files that touch history.state/pushState/replaceState/back throw at the top level (3 HARNESS_ERROR files) or fail on `.state` (history-state.window.js, 2).
- Spec: HTML 7.2 History interface (7.2.1-7.2.4: pushState/replaceState 'shared history push/replace state steps'), 8.4.2 document open steps (history entry untouched)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/window/window.cpp (History interface object + window.history); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp (a frame's own history); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/lifecycle.cpp (a session-history list per browsing context: url + state)
- Evidence: html/webappapis/dynamic-markup-insertion/html-unsafe-methods/Document-parseHTMLUnsafe-url-pushstate.html (HARNESS_ERROR: 'history is not defined'); html/webappapis/dynamic-markup-insertion/html-unsafe-methods/Document-parseHTMLUnsafe-url-base-pushstate.html (HARNESS_ERROR); html/webappapis/dynamic-markup-insertion/opening-the-input-stream/history.window.js (HARNESS_ERROR, also window.open); [..]
- Fix: A minimal History per bindings: {length, state, scrollRestoration, pushState(data,unused,url), replaceState, back/forward/go -> no-op or fragment-only}, pushState/replaceState structuredClone the state and update location_href_ (same-origin, resolve against the document URL) and `document.URL`; document.open keeps `history.state` (8.4.2 step 15 does not touch it). ~120 lines in a new bindings/window/history.cpp; the interface object via the existing interface_object helper.

### Inline event handler scope omits the form owner for <img> and form-associated custom elements

- **2 subtests / 1 files**, size S, overlaps CE2, confidence high
- Root cause: compile_handler_attribute (ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/dispatch.cpp:1418-1430) computes `with_form` only for button/fieldset/input/object/output/select/textarea; HTML's 'form-associated elements' list also has img and form-associated custom elements, so `<img onclick="elements">` resolves to the global (got "global_elements").
- Spec: HTML 8.1.8.1 event handler content attributes step 10.3 (form owner scope), 4.10.2 form-associated elements (includes img and form-associated custom elements)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/dispatch.cpp (compile_handler_attribute with_form list); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/custom_elements.cpp (formAssociated flag lookup)
- Evidence: html/webappapis/scripting/events/compile-event-handler-lexical-scopes-form-owner.html (2: '<img> has a form owner', 'form-associated <x-foo> has a form owner')
- Fix: Add `img` to the list and, for a custom element, consult the registry's definition `formAssociated`; both then read `form` (the img needs a `form` owner accessor - HTML 4.10.17 form owner for img is the nearest ancestor form).

### A throw inside a microtask is swallowed as a VM failure: the value is lost and the rest of the queue is dropped

- **1 subtests / 1 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: context::drain_microtasks (ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/script/vm.hpp:855-868) calls `call(job.fn, job.args)`, not call_fenced: an uncaught throw sets `failed_`, the loop stops ('!failed_'), the remaining jobs stay queued/dropped (run.cpp:86 'A FAILED SCRIPT'S QUEUE DIES WITH IT'), and the host only ever sees the text via note_callback_fault('microtask') -> dispatch_error(message) with error=undefined, so `ev.error === error` fails. HTML says each microtask's exception is reported (to the callback's global) and the checkpoint continues.
- Spec: HTML 8.1.7.3 perform a microtask checkpoint step 2.6 (report the exception), 8.1.3.3 report an exception
- Engine files: ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/script/vm.hpp (drain_microtasks); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/window/callbacks.cpp (report through dispatch_error_value with the thrown value); ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/call/run.cpp
- Evidence: html/webappapis/microtask-queuing/queue-microtask-exceptions.any.js (1: 'expected Error: boo but got undefined'); html/webappapis/microtask-queuing/queue-microtask-cross-realm-callback-report-exception.html (1, needs REALM too)
- Fix: drain_microtasks: run each job through call_fenced(fn, args, undefined, threw, thrown); on threw (not on a VM fault) invoke a host hook `report_exception_(thrown)` (a std::function beside rejection_tracker_, set by dom_bindings to dispatch_error_value(describe_thrown, thrown)) and CONTINUE the drain; only a genuine `failed_` (resource ceiling) stops it. Promise reaction jobs already catch their own throws, so this only changes queueMicrotask/MutationObserver jobs.

**Long tail:** Cross-origin www1 host tests (8 files / 12 subtests: runtime-error-cross-origin*, compile-error-cross-origin*, allow/disallow-crossorigin - need a second origin and CORS the file: runner cannot serve; muted 'Script error.' reporting would follow once it can). Worker/SharedWorker absent (5 files / 10 subtests + 2 HARNESS_ERROR .dedicatedworker files). window.open (5 files: document-write/047, document.open-02, reload, history.window.js, promise-job-entry sanity). window.print/stop/focus (user-prompts 3, update-the-rendering-resize-autofocus 1). navigator.oscpu/userAgent 'value' TIMEOUT (2) and [..]

**Skipped (already fixed at HEAD):** Already addressed at HEAD (b722aa41), do not re-count: (1) unhandledrejection/rejectionhandled - cced0f19 added lib/Shell/bindings/promise_rejections.cpp + PromiseRejectionEvent (interfaces.cpp:724) after the baseline: promise-rejection-events.html (22), -many-promises (6), -onerror (1), -attached-in-event (1), tasks.window.js 'Promise rejection' (2) - BUT the subtests that use the file's queueTask() helper (a disconnected <details> with `open` set) stay TIMEOUT until the toggle-task cluster above lands. (2) requestAnimationFrame: cancelAnimationFrame, cancel-during-run and exception [..]

## html-dom — html/dom + html/semantics/forms (the WPT checkout only carries html/semantics/forms; interactive/embedded/tabular/text-level are not in the corpus)

Baseline: /tmp/m-9f8da347/html-dom.json: 376 files = 153 PASS / 71 FAIL / 13 TIMEOUT / 2 HARNESS_ERROR / 137 SKIP; subtests 60,524 PASS / 191 non-PASS. /tmp/w-9f8da347/html.json filtered to html/dom/** + html/semantics/**: 1,024 files = 316 PASS / 212 FAIL / 34 TIMEOUT / 10 HARNESS_ERROR / 452 SKIP; subtests 63,976 PASS / 1,409 non-PASS, of which forms 1,218, render-blocking 59, global-attributes 54, [..]

### Echo-server (.py handler) dependent submissions: no wptserve, POST never navigates, no multipart/text-plain body serialisers

- **202 subtests / 5 files**, size L, overlaps none, confidence medium
- Root cause: tools/wpt/run-wpt.py has no server (its own header: 'there is no server here'); `/` paths are read off CTBROWSER_DOC_ROOT (assets.hpp set_document_root), so a POST to /FileAPI/file/resources/echo-content-escaped.py or /fetch/api/resources/echo-content.py can never echo the entity body - the frame document is empty and every assert reads ''. navigate_form_target additionally refuses method=post (frames.cpp:455 `if (method == "post" || method == "dialog") return false;`) and only serialize_form_urlencoded exists - no multipart/form-data or text/plain encoder (4.10.21.7/8).
- Spec: HTML 4.10.21.3 steps 21-22, 4.10.21.7 multipart/form-data encoding, 4.10.21.8 text/plain encoding
- Engine files: tools/wpt/run-wpt.py; ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/shell/page/assets.hpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/multipart-formdata.window.js (62/62); html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/text-plain.window.js (62/62); html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/urlencoded2.window.js (62/62); html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/submit-entity-body.html (13, TIMEOUT); html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/form-data-set-empty-file.window.js (3 of 4)
- Fix: Two halves. Engine: in navigate_form_target accept POST and build the request body with the enctype's serialiser (multipart/form-data per 4.10.21.7 with a random boundary, text/plain per 4.10.21.8, urlencoded via URLSearchParams' rules) and hand {url, method, body, content-type} to load_frame. Infrastructure (pick one): (a) run-wpt.py launches `wpt serve` and the registry fetches http://web-platform.test:8000 for anything under the doc root that is a .py handler; or (b) the cheap in-engine emulation - when the planned navigation's path is one of the three echo handlers (echo-content.py, echo-content-escaped.py with its \xNN escaping, form-submission.py), the frame's document is the request body as text/plain. 33 of these subtests ALSO need the DataTransfer cluster.

### <input type=color>: colorspace/alpha attributes not threaded through sanitisation nor reflected

- **182 subtests / 1 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: d69dee50 (round seven) added sanitize_color(value, display_p3, alpha) but input_types.cpp's colour branch calls it for the limited-sRGB opaque form only, and reflection.cpp has no `colorSpace`/`alpha` on HTMLInputElement - the commit body says so explicitly. Every color.window.js case with color space 'display-p3' or 'with alpha' therefore serialises `#rrggbb` instead of `color(srgb r g b / a)` / `color(display-p3 ...)`, and re-sanitisation on attribute change does not happen.
- Spec: HTML 4.10.5.1.15 Color state (colorspace, alpha, 'update a color well control color'), CSS Color 4 serialisation
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/page/input_types.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/reflection.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/color.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/page/forms.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/color.window.js (182/196: 'Actual: #000000 Expected: color(srgb 0 0 0)', 'Expected: color(display-p3 0 0 0)', 'value is hex expected #ffffff but got #000000')
- Fix: input_types sanitized(): read the element's `colorspace` (enumerated limited-srgb|display-p3, default limited-srgb) and `alpha` (boolean) and pass them to sanitize_color; add enum_attr("HTMLInputElement","colorSpace","colorspace","limited-srgb display-p3","limited-srgb","limited-srgb") and bool_attr("HTMLInputElement","alpha") in the reflection table; re-run sanitisation when either attribute changes (the same hook settle_types uses for `type`). The unit test in css_values_color.cpp already covers the serialiser.

### Form submission algorithm: no planned navigation, submitter dropped, SubmitEvent/FormDataEvent absent, form* attributes ignored

- **127 subtests / 14 files**, size L, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: HEAD (456b2a5b) navigates a GET-to-named-frame SYNCHRONOUSLY inside build_submission (control_methods.cpp:2733-2755 -> frames.cpp:443-480 load_frame), so there is no 'planned navigation' that a later submit()/click supersedes (HTML step 24: 'v4' wins; the engine loads the frame three times and the first load resolves loadPromise). browser::submit (actions.cpp:214-230) fires `submit` as a plain Event via dispatch("submit") with no `submitter` and calls submit_form_(form, node_id{}) - the clicked button's formaction/formtarget/formmethod/formenctype and its name=value entry are lost (navigate_form_target reads only the form's attributes, its own comment says so). No SubmitEvent or [..]
- Spec: HTML 4.10.21.3 (steps 5-7, 17-20, 24 'plan to navigate'), 4.10.21.4, 4.10.22 SubmitEvent, 4.10.21.8 FormDataEvent, 4.10.6 button command
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/actions.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/dispatch.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/interfaces.cpp; ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/shell/bindings.hpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/form-double-submit-requestsubmit.html (36, HARNESS_ERROR; 'expected v2 but got v1', 'Invalid URL: undefined'); html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/form-double-submit-input-type-change.html (20); html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/input-type-change-submit.html (20); html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/form-submit-action.html (10: formaction/form= [..]
- Fix: (1) Carry the submitter: on_activate_ -> browser::activate -> browser::submit(form, submitter); submit_form_(form, submitter). (2) Define SubmitEvent (submitter) and FormDataEvent (formData) constructors in interfaces.cpp/events and fire `submit` as a SubmitEvent from both requestSubmit and the activation path. (3) In navigate_form_target take action/method/enctype/target from the submitter's form* attributes first (4.10.21.3 steps 17-20), choose the navigable per 'rules for choosing a navigable' incl. empty/_self = the form's own navigable (a frame's dom_bindings navigates its own element via the parent's load_frame). (4) Replace the immediate load_frame with a `planned_navigation{form, navigable, url}` member; a new plan for the same form overwrites it; drain it from the task loop (where timers run) - that alone fixes every double-submit file. (5) Keep the form's [..]

### Constraint-validation flags are zeroed when the control is barred (disabled/readonly)

- **106 subtests / 10 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: validity_flags (control_methods.cpp:2107-2111) returns early: `if (!will_validate(txn, b, id)) { return flags; }` with a comment claiming the browsers agree. They do not: validator.js's iterate_over clones each control with disabled=true and expects the same flags ('expectedImmutable' defaults to expected); only validationMessage/checkValidity/willValidate depend on being barred.
- Spec: HTML 4.10.20.1 definitions ('suffering from ...' are defined on the value; 'barred from constraint validation' only excludes the element from checkValidity/reportValidate/willValidate)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/constraints/form-validation-validity-valid.html (24: 'validity.valid should be false, when control is disabled'); html/semantics/forms/constraints/form-validation-validity-patternMismatch.html (22 of 43); html/semantics/forms/constraints/form-validation-validity-rangeOverflow.html (16 of 19), -rangeUnderflow.html (16 of 18); [..]
- Fix: Delete the early return in validity_flags; keep `will_validate` for willValidate, checkValidity/reportValidity (skip barred controls) and validationMessage (empty when barred, which validator.js also asserts). One-line root-cause fix; the per-type message counts follow.

### Render-blocking: the parser is not incremental, so rendering opportunities never interleave with parsing

- **59 subtests / 38 files**, size L, overlaps none, confidence medium
- Root cause: Every render-blocking test parses a document whose inline scripts spin (generateParserDelay) and asserts from a requestAnimationFrame that later markup (#last) has NOT been parsed yet, or that rendering is blocked until a `blocking=render` script/link or `<link rel=expect>` settles. The engine parses the whole document before the first frame, so rAF callbacks see the complete DOM ('assert_false: expected false got true' in 35 files) and nothing implements the blocking attribute / rel=expect at all (rg 'blocking' hits only views.cpp/performance.cpp).
- Spec: HTML 8.1.7.3 event loop processing model (rendering opportunities), 4.2.7 blocking attribute + render-blocking mechanism, 4.6.7 rel=expect
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/frame.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/entry.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/DOM/treebuilder.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/window/callbacks.cpp
- Evidence: html/dom/render-blocking/element-render-blocking-002..036.html (35 files x 1: 'blocking defers until needed element is parsed', 'removing blocking makes it no longer blocking'); html/dom/render-blocking/remove-attr-unblocks-rendering.optional.html (9, TIMEOUT); html/dom/render-blocking/non-render-blocking-scripts.optional.html (7, TIMEOUT), remove-element-unblocks-rendering.optional.html (7); [..]
- Fix: Make the parser resumable: after each parser-inserted script executes, return to the event loop (run microtasks, due timers, and - if the document's render-blocking element set is empty - a rendering opportunity with rAF callbacks) before continuing tokenisation. Track the render-blocking set: `<script blocking=render>`/`<link blocking=render>` in <head>, `<link rel=expect href=#id>` until #id is parsed; removal of the element/attribute or a non-matching media unblocks. This is the html/syntax agent's parser-driven-document work more than forms; listed because the files sit in html/dom.

### DataTransfer missing and input.files is always null (no FileList)

- **46 subtests / 8 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: `accessor("HTMLInputElement","files", ...)` at control_methods.cpp:1964 returns value::null() unconditionally and has no setter; `DataTransfer` is not defined anywhere in lib/Shell (rg), so every file-submission test that builds its File via `new DataTransfer(); dt.items.add(file); input.files = dt.files` rejects with ReferenceError. The entry list also omits the empty File an empty <input type=file> must contribute.
- Spec: HTML 4.10.5.1.18 File Upload state (files getter/setter), 4.10.21.4 constructing the entry list step 5.7, HTML 6.11.2 DataTransfer / 6.11.3 DataTransferItemList
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/interfaces.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/xhr.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/{multipart-formdata,text-plain,urlencoded2}.window.js (11 each = 33: 'ReferenceError: DataTransfer is not defined'; these also need the echo-server cluster); html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/newline-normalization.html (4), constructing-form-data-set.html (3), submit-file.sub.html (1); html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/files.html (3: 'got [..]
- Fix: A FileList wrapper (array-like, item(), length) held in a per-input slot; `files` getter returns it (empty, not null) for type=file and null otherwise; setter accepts only a FileList (TypeError otherwise, ignored when not type=file). DataTransfer constructor with `items` (DataTransferItemList.add(File)/add(string,type), remove, clear) and `files` (a FileList of the File items), dropEffect/effectAllowed/types. Entry list: a type=file with no files appends File("", type application/octet-stream). Reuse the File/Blob objects xhr.cpp already builds.

### Text control selection is seeded at the end of the value, and moved by things that must not move it

- **38 subtests / 4 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: form_store::state_of seeds `seeded.caret = seeded.value.size()` (forms.cpp:123) and, when the value attribute is re-read for a non-dirty control, sets the caret to the end again (forms.cpp:92); a type change clamps instead of resetting (forms.cpp:55); textarea child mutations re-seed the raw value the same way; set_value (forms.cpp:159) compares the un-normalised text so setting the same CRLF value counts as a change and moves the cursor. The spec's selection is 0/0 until the user or the `value` IDL setter moves it.
- Spec: HTML 4.10.19.5 text control selection (selectionStart/End defaults), 4.10.5.1 'value' mode setter (move the text entry cursor to the end), 4.10.11 textarea 'relevant value' / child text content [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/page/forms.cpp; ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/shell/page/forms.hpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/type-change-state.html (25 of 380: 'selectionStart should be 0 expected 0 but got 2/6/7'); html/semantics/forms/textfieldselection/selection-start-end-extra.html (6: 'Setting textContent in a textarea should NOT move selection', 'expected 0 but got 6'); html/semantics/forms/textfieldselection/defaultSelection.html (4: 'expected 0 but got 3'); [..]
- Fix: Seed caret = selection = 0; on attribute re-seed and textarea child changes clamp the existing selection to the new length instead of moving it (the spec only says the value changed, not the cursor); on a type change set the selection to 0/0 (what every browser and type-change-state expect); in set_value compare against the normalised API value (LF-only for textarea) and only then move the cursor to the end. control_methods' selection_of already reads through code units, so nothing else moves.

### Scripts do not run in frames (REALM)

- **28 subtests / 7 files**, size L, overlaps REALM, confidence high
- Root cause: Tests whose assertion is made by a document loaded into an <iframe> (form-with-action.sub.html calling parent.success, the currentScript frame, the self-origin sub-frames, show-picker cross-origin frames) never get there because a frame shares the page realm and its scripts never execute.
- Spec: HTML 7.1 browsing contexts / realms, 7.7 postMessage
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/the-form-element/form-action-submission.html (3, TIMEOUT), form-action-submission-with-base-url.html (3, TIMEOUT); html/dom/self-origin.sub.html (10, HARNESS_ERROR: postMessage undefined), self-origin.any.js (1); html/dom/documents/dom-tree-accessors/Document.currentScript.html (3, TIMEOUT); html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/show-picker-cross-origin-iframe.html (4), [..]
- Fix: Covered by round8-brief-REALM.md (one realm per browsing context, frame scripts run, postMessage).

### dir=auto ignores form control values, non-HTML script/style, CDATA, and text mutations do not restyle

- **27 subtests / 9 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: engine::direction_is_rtl/first_strong (lib/Style/engine.cpp:2297-2340) read only child text nodes: an <input dir=auto> / <textarea dir=auto> must use its value (the store's, or the value attribute) and does not; the skip of script/style/textarea does not check element_ns (non-HTML ones must count); is_text_kind may exclude CDATA (cdata-dir_auto TIMEOUT); and a text insertion into a dir=auto subtree does not invalidate the ancestor's :dir()/direction. control_methods.cpp:2523-2524 hard-codes the dirname entry to "ltr" instead of the element's directionality.
- Spec: HTML 3.2.6.4 auto-directionality (text-form-control directionality, contained-text auto directionality), 4.10.18.2 dirname
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/mutation.cpp
- Evidence: html/dom/elements/global-attributes/dir-auto-form-associated.window.js (11 of 22: '<input dir=auto type=text> directionality'); html/dom/elements/global-attributes/cdata-dir_auto.html (3, TIMEOUT), dir-assorted.window.js (3: 'non-html style element text contents influence dir=auto'); html/dom/elements/global-attributes/dir-auto-dynamic-changes.window.js (3 non-slot: 'CSSdirection after insertion [..]
- Fix: In first_strong: for an HTML input in a text-ish type or a textarea with dir=auto, run first_strong_in over the control's value (a Style->Shell hook, or the value attribute + a store callback); skip bdi/script/style/textarea only when element_ns==html; include CDATA via is_text_kind. Mark the nearest dir=auto ancestor's style dirty on any text/child mutation and on control value writes (the wrote_to_control_ path). dirname: replace the literal "ltr" with direction_is_rtl(element) ? "rtl" : "ltr" and apply dirname only where it applies (text/search/tel/url/email/password/hidden/submit/reset/button + textarea).

### pattern attribute falls back to the `u` flag when `v` fails, so invalid-under-v patterns still constrain

- **23 subtests / 2 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: compile_pattern (control_methods.cpp:2074-2095) tries `new RegExp('^(?:'+p+')$','v')` and then FALLS BACK to the 'u' flag; the spec compiles with v only and an error means 'no constraint'. `[(]` and `a)(b` are errors under v (ClassSetSyntaxCharacter / stray paren) but compile under u, so the value mismatches; the JavaScript-specific cases (`\cx[5-\[]{2}`) suggest ctjs's v-mode class parsing (round seven's J2) also diverges.
- Spec: HTML 4.10.5.3.6 pattern (compiled pattern: 'v' flag, ^(?: ... )$), ECMA-262 22.2.1 ClassSetCharacter
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/constraints/form-validation-validity-patternMismatch.html (21 of 43: 'Invalid `v` regular expression gets ignored', 'The pattern attribute tries to escape a group', 'matches JavaScript-specific regular expression' -> 'patternMismatch should be false'); html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/pattern_attribute_v_flag.html (2: '<input pattern="[(]" value="foo"> should be valid')
- Fix: Remove the 'u' fallback in the compiled helper (v or nothing). Then check ctjs's v-mode class parser rejects unescaped ClassSetSyntaxCharacters `( ) [ ] { } / - \ |` inside [...] and accepts `\cx`, `[5-\[]` and `\q{...}` string literals; whatever still fails is a ctjs regexp bug for the JS agent.

### cloneNode drops `indeterminate` (a wrapper slot, not store state)

- **22 subtests / 1 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: tree_ops.cpp:100-113 clones the form store's control_state (value, checkedness, dirty flags - b8ec8864/d813ab52) but `indeterminate` lives in the wrapper slot `__indeterminate` (control_methods.cpp:1966-1980), which no clone step copies.
- Spec: HTML 4.10.5 input element cloning steps (browsers and the test include indeterminate; whatwg/html#3567)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/tree_ops.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp; ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/shell/page/forms.hpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/cloning-steps.html (22 of 68: 'input[type=X] element's indeterminateness should be cloned' for every type)
- Fix: Move `indeterminate` into control_state (bool) so clone_state carries it for free, or copy the `__indeterminate` slot in the clone path next to the store copy.

### dir/lang through slots and shadow hosts

- **21 subtests / 3 files**, size S, overlaps FLAT, confidence high
- Root cause: direction_is_rtl walks txn.parent() and first_strong walks txn.children() - the light tree. A slot's directionality comes from its assigned nodes and a slotted element's from the host/slot chain, i.e. the flat tree; language_of has the same gap (lang on host).
- Spec: HTML 3.2.6.4 (uses the flat tree: 'assigned nodes', 'host'), 3.2.6.2 lang
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp
- Evidence: html/dom/elements/global-attributes/dir-slots-directionality.html (9 of 17: 'Expect rtl for slot with assigned rtl content'); html/dom/elements/global-attributes/dir-auto-dynamic-changes.window.js (10 slot cases: 'dir=auto changes for slot reassignment', 'Slotted content affects multiple dir=auto slots'); html/dom/elements/global-attributes/lang-attribute-shadow.window.js (2: 'lang only on host')
- Fix: Once the FLAT brief's flat-tree parent/children accessors exist, point direction_is_rtl/first_strong/language_of at them (assigned nodes for a slot, the host for a shadow root).

### Radio button group: the checked setter never unchecks the group, and toggle walks the whole document by name only

- **12 subtests / 2 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: The HTMLInputElement `checked` setter (control_methods.cpp:1950-1961) writes state.checked and nothing else; form_store::toggle (forms.cpp:248-266) walks from txn.root() (so a disconnected tree or a different form owner is either missed or wrongly swept) and matches only by name attribute. The group is: same tree root (incl. disconnected subtrees and fragments), same form owner, same non-empty name.
- Spec: HTML 4.10.5.1.16 Radio Button state (radio button group definition, checkedness rules)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/page/forms.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/radio.html (7 of 12: 'only one control of a radio button group can have its checkedness set to true', 'Radio buttons in an orphan tree should make a group'); html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/radio-disconnected-group-owner.html (5 of 8: 'Removed elements are moved into separate radio groups', 'Disconnected document fragments can serve as radio group [..]
- Fix: One form_store::uncheck_group(txn, atoms, id, owner) walking from the element's own root (root_of_tree) and comparing name + form owner; call it from toggle, from the `checked` setter when the new value is true, and from the name/form-attribute change and insertion steps (4.10.5.1.16's 'when the element's checkedness becomes true'). validity_flags' radio walk (control_methods.cpp:2169-2190) already has the right group test - lift it into the store.

### HTMLFormElement has no named getter

- **11 subtests / 1 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: form.button / form.l1 / form['addEventListener'] are undefined: rg finds no named-property hook on the form wrapper (wrapper.cpp / control_methods.cpp only define `elements`, `length`, submit/reset); document/named_access.cpp has the document-level pattern but nothing for forms, and there is no RadioNodeList for the multi-match case.
- Spec: HTML 4.10.3 The form element (named getter, past names map), 4.10.3.1 RadioNodeList
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/wrapper.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/named_access.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/the-form-element/form-nameditem.html (11 of 17: 'Name for a single element should work', 'Named elements should override builtins', 'Calling item() on the NodeList returned from the named getter')
- Fix: On the form wrapper's property-miss path (the same hook named_access.cpp uses for the document): collect elements_of_form(form) plus <img> descendants whose id or name matches (past-names map for a removed element); one match -> the element, several -> a RadioNodeList (a live NodeList with `value` = the checked radio's value). Named properties shadow the prototype ('override builtins').

### Form pseudo-classes parsed but never matched; :disabled ignores optgroup/fieldset ancestry

- **11 subtests / 3 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: selector.cpp:40-58 structural_bit_of maps only root/empty/*-child/*-of-type/disabled/enabled/checked/link/visited/scope/defined; :valid, :invalid, :required, :optional, :in-range, :out-of-range, :indeterminate, :placeholder-shown are in known_pseudo_class (parse OK) but have no bit, so they never match. engine.cpp:647 `is_disabled = own disabled attribute` - an <option> inside <optgroup disabled> (even through a wrapper <div>) and a control inside a disabled <fieldset> are not :disabled.
- Spec: Selectors 4 §13 input pseudo-classes; HTML 4.10.5.1.16 / 4.10.9 / 4.10.15 'actually disabled' (optgroup, fieldset)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/selector.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/pattern_attribute.html (4: 'basic <input pattern> support' asserts input.matches(':invalid')); html/semantics/forms/the-select-element/customizable-select/option-disabled-optgroup.html (4: 'options should check ancestor optgroup for disabled state'); html/semantics/forms/the-select-element/customizable-select/option-disabled-optgroup-wrapper.html (3: 'Option [..]
- Fix: Add state bits for the eight pseudo-classes and fill them from a Shell hook that calls validity_flags/will_validate (control_methods.cpp:2093) - the same route state_checked already takes; :disabled = own attribute, or an <optgroup disabled> ancestor for option, or a disabled <fieldset> ancestor outside its first <legend>. Invalidate on value/attribute writes (wrote_to_control_).

### Textarea raw value vs API value: no newline normalisation, no wrap=hard, defaultValue uses textContent

- **9 subtests / 2 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: state_of seeds a textarea from `txn.text(child)` of direct children (forms.cpp:110-113) and the `value` getter hands that raw value back - the API value must have CRLF and CR normalised to LF, and the 'wrapping transformation' (wrap=hard + cols) is not applied at all. `defaultValue` (control_methods.cpp:1697-1712) is text_content()/set_text() - the spec says child text content (direct Text children only) and 'string replace all'.
- Spec: HTML 4.10.11 The textarea element (raw value, API value, wrapping transformation, defaultValue)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/page/forms.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/the-textarea-element/value-defaultValue-textContent.html (6: 'The value property should normalize CRLF and CR to LF expected foo\nbar but got foo\r\nbar', 'defaultValue should reflect the child text content expected foo bar but got foo barbaz'); html/semantics/forms/the-textarea-element/wrapping-transformation.window.js (3: 'Newlines should be normalized to LF', [..]
- Fix: Keep the raw value in control_state; add an `api_value()` that normalises newlines (and the submission path applies the wrapping transformation for wrap=hard with cols); the `value` getter and selection maths use the API value. defaultValue getter = concatenation of direct Text children; setter = string replace all (one Text child). Reuse core/algorithms' whitespace helpers.

### email value sanitisation: multiple not split/trimmed, IDN domain not punycoded

- **7 subtests / 2 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: input_types.cpp:424-428 trims the whole value only; with `multiple` the spec splits on ',', strips whitespace from each address and rejoins with ',' (the comment there defers it to 'the caller that knows multiple', which never rewrites the value). The domain part must be converted to ASCII (url.cpp's domain_to_ascii/punycode exist and are unused here).
- Spec: HTML 4.10.5.1.5 E-mail state (value sanitization algorithm, valid e-mail address list), UTS #46 domain to ASCII
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/page/input_types.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/net/url.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/email.html (3: 'expected user@example.com,user2@example.com but got user@example.com , user2@example.com'); html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/email-value-idn.html (4: 'expected test@xn--exmle-hra.com but got test@exämle.com')
- Fix: In the email branch: split on ',' when the element has `multiple`, trim each with html_whitespace, punycode the part after the last '@' via url.cpp's domain_to_ascii, join with ','; re-sanitise when `multiple` is added/removed (settle_types-style).

### Form owner ignores the parser's form element pointer and `form=` in reset

- **5 subtests / 2 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: form_owner (control_methods.cpp:702-723) is an ancestor walk plus the `form` attribute; the tree builder records no form element pointer (rg 'form element pointer' in lib/DOM: none), so a control inside a <table> whose <form> self-closed has no owner; browser::reset -> forms_.reset_form walks the form's descendants only, so `<input form=form5>` is not reset.
- Spec: HTML 4.10.17.3 form owner (parser-inserted association), 13.2.6.4.7 'in table' insertion mode for form, 4.10.21.4 reset
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/DOM/treebuilder.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/page/forms.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/actions.cpp
- Evidence: html/semantics/forms/the-input-element/reset.html (3 of 6: 'reset button resets controls associated with a form using the form attribute' expected foobar got barfoo); html/semantics/forms/form-control-infrastructure/form.html (2: 'label-in-table-with-control.form expected form3 but got null')
- Fix: Tree builder: when the form element pointer is set and the element is form-associated with no `form` attribute, record the association (an internal attribute or a document-side map the DOM keeps); form_owner consults it before the ancestor walk. reset(): iterate elements_of_form (which already resolves `form=`) instead of the descendant walk.

**Long tail:** customizable <select> (48 subtests / 15 files: <selectedcontent>, base-appearance UA sheet, select.value with non-option parts - a new spec, needs the element + UA styles); form-submission-target rel-*-target.html (28: BroadcastChannel + window.open/opener); partial-updates/tentative (34, tentative spec); aria-attribute-reflection-enumerated.tentative (26: the tentative test expects the enumerated defaults \"none\"/\"false\", HEAD's cb3d3f88 returns null to satisfy the stable aria-attribute-reflection.html - a deliberate conflict); innerText getter (7: ::first-line/::first-letter [..]

**Skipped (already fixed at HEAD):** select-event.html (186 subtests, the slice's largest file): d813ab52 makes a cloned control's selection collapse at 0 (the 'disconnected node' / 'twice in disconnected node' cases, 'expected 0 but got 6') and 35ea1702 stops setRangeText moving the caret before the range is set (the 'a second time (must not fire)' cases); with the clone no longer fully selected the remaining timer ordering looks right, so not counted - verify in the round-seven measurement. color.html (12: 'Valid simple color' / 'leading space' cases): d69dee50 parses the value as a CSS <color> and serialises the colour well - [..]

## html-parsing — html/syntax + domparsing + selection + url (html/browsers, html/infrastructure, html/editing and encoding/ were NOT in the wide run: /tmp/w-9f8da347/html.json holds only html/{semantics,dom,webappapis,syntax}; no encoding.json exists)

Baseline: 615 files in slice at 9f8da347: 303 PASS, 155 FAIL, 133 SKIP (reftests/crashtests/testdriver), 16 HARNESS_ERROR, 8 TIMEOUT; 54,308 subtests, 3,011 failing. Per suite (files pass/total, failing subtests/total): domparsing 14/72, 1350/1612; html/syntax 209/311, 255/8695; selection 41/183, 740/34066; url 39/49, 666/9935.

### streamHTML/streamAppendHTML/... (WritableStream-fed streaming parser) does not exist

- **754 subtests / 29 files**, size L, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: The same tentative proposal's streaming half: `element.streamHTML()` returns a WritableStream whose chunks drive an incremental HTML parser at a position, with sanitizer, custom-element reaction, parser-blocking-script and declarative-shadow-root semantics. The engine has no WritableStream at all (rg WritableStream in lib/Script and lib/Shell: nothing), no incremental tokenizer entry point (parse_html_fragment is one-shot) and no Sanitizer.
- Spec: WICG declarative-partial-updates streamHTML; Streams Standard §4 WritableStream; HTML §13.2 parser re-entrancy
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/shadow_dom.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/DOM/tokenizer.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/DOM/treebuilder.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/
- Evidence: domparsing/tentative/stream-*.html: 754 failing subtests across 29 files (stream-html-sanitizer 192, stream-positional 82, stream-sanitizer-img-ctor 65, stream-fragment-context 54, stream-insertion-logic 52, stream-html-with-declarative-shadow-root 36, stream-tokenizer-context 32, ...), every one "`streamHTML` is undefined, not a function"; 6 more stream-html-with-trusted-types-*.html files are [..]
- Fix: Only worth it as a whole: (1) WritableStream/WritableStreamDefaultWriter in the VM builtins (Streams §4), (2) a resumable tokenizer+tree-builder (the document.write path already re-enters the parser at </script>; expose 'feed(chunk)' / 'close()' on a tree_builder held by the element), (3) the positional insertion of the previous cluster, (4) the Sanitizer default config. L, and tentative - recommend deferring; note it in wpt.md as 'tentative, not planned'.

### URL parse failure never throws at XMLHttpRequest.open, window.open (missing) and Location assignment

- **570 subtests / 1 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: url/failure.html feeds 190 unparseable absolute URLs (file://example:1/, http://user:pass@/, https://%EF%BF%BD ...) to three APIs and expects SyntaxError from each. (1) xhr.cpp:186-189 `open` bypasses the parser whenever the input contains "://" (`given.find("://") != npos ? given : resolve(...)`) and net/url.cpp:1702 `resolve` is deliberately lenient - it returns the reference unchanged on failure - so `url.empty()` at xhr.cpp:193 never fires. (2) `window.open` does not exist (no "open" global in lib/Shell/bindings/window/*, subtest message "`open` is undefined"). (3) A frame window's `location` is a plain data property (frames.cpp:379 `frame_window->set("location", made.location_)`, same [..]
- Spec: XHR §4.5.6 open() steps 7-8; HTML §7.2.2.1 window.open() steps; HTML §7.10.1 Location href setter; URL §4.4 basic URL parser failure
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/xhr.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/window/window.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/net/url.cpp
- Evidence: url/failure.html: 570/1175 failing = 190 "XHR: ... should throw" (assert_throws_dom did not throw) + 190 "Location's href: ... should throw" (did not throw) + 190 "window.open(): ... should throw" (TypeError `open` is undefined)
- Fix: xhr.cpp open: replace the "://" shortcut with `parse_url(given, location_href_)` and throw DOMException SyntaxError on nullopt (XHR 4.5.6 steps 7-8). Add `window.open(url='', target, features)` (HTML 7.2.2.1 window open steps): parse url against the document base URL, failure -> SyntaxError; return null (engine cannot open windows) or a stub with `close()` - the test only needs the throw. Make `location` an accessor on both the page window and the frame window (frames.cpp:379, window install) whose setter runs Location's href setter (HTML 7.10.1 step 3-5: parse against the entry base, failure -> SyntaxError, else navigate the frame - frames.cpp already has a navigate path from 456b2a5b). sendBeacon passes by accident (undefined -> TypeError); leave it.

### Positional HTML methods (appendHTML/prependHTML/beforeHTML/afterHTML/replaceWithHTML/replaceChildrenHTML + Unsafe) do not exist

- **360 subtests / 4 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: WICG declarative-partial-updates (tentative). `object[method_name]` is undefined on Element, ShadowRoot and CharacterData ("`a computed member` is undefined, not a function" x336; the 12 assert_throws cases expected HierarchyRequestError for a comment/template-child target and got the TypeError). The engine already has both halves: setHTMLUnsafe/getHTML with declarative shadow roots (shadow_dom.cpp:317) and insertAdjacentHTML's position logic (node_methods.cpp:163-230, `adjacent_place`).
- Spec: WICG declarative-partial-updates (https://github.com/WICG/declarative-partial-updates); HTML setHTMLUnsafe; DOM Parsing insertAdjacentHTML; Sanitizer API default config
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/node_methods.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/shadow_dom.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/interfaces.cpp
- Evidence: domparsing/tentative/positional-methods.html 348/348; domparsing/tentative/positional-template-child.html 12/12 (HierarchyRequestError on a template's direct child); domparsing/tentative/positional-methods-with-parser-options.html + -with-trusted-types.html: HARNESS_ERROR (`trustedTypes` is not defined)
- Fix: One installer over the six positions: parse markup as a fragment with context = the target's parent for before/after/replaceWith (the target itself for append/prepend/replaceChildren), attach declarative shadow roots for the Unsafe variants, then reuse insertAdjacentHTML's placement (beforebegin/afterbegin/beforeend/afterend + replace). The 'safe' variants apply the Sanitizer default config (drop <script>, on* attributes, javascript: URLs) and never run scripts; Unsafe runs scripts only with `{runScripts: true}` in the second argument. Throw HierarchyRequestError when the target's parent is a template content / null for the sibling positions. Tentative spec - decide whether it earns a session before spending one; the trustedTypes files stay HARNESS_ERROR without a Trusted Types stub.

### Frame scripts run in the page's realm: iframe-driven tests in this slice (Selection, DOMParser, parser reparenting, html5lib scripted variants)

- **202 subtests / 32 files**, size L, overlaps REALM, confidence high
- Root cause: frames.cpp header says it: a frame runs none of its own scripts and contentWindow is a proxy over the page's globals. So `iframe.contentWindow.DOMParser` is undefined (DOMParser-parseFromString-url*: "Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'parseFromString')", "`doParse` is undefined"), a made-in-frame document cannot get the frame document's URL ("Child window ... expected file:///common/blank.html?1 but got about:blank"), deleteFromDocument.html's iframe defines `run` ("`run` is undefined, not a function - in `initializeIframe`"), getSelection.html wants a distinct Selection per frame window, adoption-agency-reparenting*/custom-element-reparenting*/foster-parenting-* use [..]
- Spec: HTML §8.1.3 realms/settings objects; HTML §8.6.2 DOMParser (URL = relevant global's associated Document's URL); Selection API §getSelection per window
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/nested.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/scripts.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/selection.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/second_document.cpp
- Evidence: selection/deleteFromDocument.html 58 (`run` is undefined in initializeIframe); domparsing/DOMParser-parseFromString-url-base.html 27 + -url.html 27 + -url-moretests.html 43 realm/iframe subtests (of 45/45/31): parseFromString on iframe DOMParser, Child window URL; selection/getSelection.html 6 (iframe's document.getSelection() null / same object as the page's); [..]
- Fix: Covered by round8-brief-REALM: a realm per frame document whose window IS the global object; frames.cpp runs the frame's inline/src scripts under it. Once that lands, also give each frame realm its own Selection instance (install_selection runs per realm) and make DOMParser-made documents take the URL of the realm's document (second_document.cpp:206-219 already reads maker.location_href_).

### selectAllChildren (and Range node arguments) refuse a Document because documents are proxy objects

- **185 subtests / 2 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: selection.cpp:305-311 `selectAllChildren` does `if (!node.is_object()) throw TypeError("parameter 1 is not of type 'Node'")`. `document`, a createHTMLDocument and a DOMParser document are `proxy_object`s - value.hpp:141 `is_object()` is `is_kind(heap_kind::object)` and false for them; value.hpp:150 `is_object_like()` is the check that admits a proxy. The very next line already handles the document case (`is_the_document(node) ? doc_->document_node()`), it is just never reached. The same shape rejects an iframe's contentDocument in Range methods ("Range: the argument is not a Node" in move-selection-range-into-different-root's sub-document cases, range.cpp node-argument check).
- Spec: Selection API §selectAllChildren(node) steps 1-6; DOM §5.5 Range set the start/end
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/selection.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/range.cpp; ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/script/value.hpp
- Evidence: selection/selectAllChildren.html: 177/2242 failing, all with node 11 document / node 14 foreignDoc / node 16 xmlDoc (59 ranges x 3 documents): "parameter 1 is not of type 'Node'"; selection/move-selection-range-into-different-root.tentative.html: 8 sub-document subtests "Range: the argument is not a Node"
- Fix: selection.cpp:307: `if (!node.is_object_like() || (!is_a_document(node) && owner_of(node) == nullptr))`; resolve a document via owner_of(node)->doc_->document_node() rather than only `is_the_document`. Audit the Range node-argument checks in range.cpp for the same `is_object()` test and use `is_a_document`/`owner_of` there too (the sub-document case then falls into the 'root is not this document -> abort' path of the next cluster).

### Selection.modify() is not implemented

- **168 subtests / 15 files**, size L, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: selection.cpp:25 says so ("ponytail: ... no `modify()`"). Every bidi/contenteditable/modify-* file calls getSelection().modify(alter, direction, granularity) and dies with "`modify` is undefined, not a function". Needs caret positions from layout: character/word/line/lineboundary/paragraph/paragraphboundary movement, 'left'/'right' resolved through bidi/visual order, and editing-host limits (modify must not leave the focused editing host).
- Spec: Selection API §modify() (legacy, WebKit-defined: https://w3c.github.io/selection-api/#dom-selection-modify); CSS Writing Modes for bidi visual order
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/selection.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/selection.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/input.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Layout/
- Evidence: selection/bidi/modify.tentative.html 36, modify-extend-by-character 28, modify-move-by-character 28; selection/caret-position-should-be-correct-while-moveup-movedown.html 24; selection/contenteditable/modify-around-non-editable-span.html 16, modify.tentative.html 15, selection/modify.tentative.html 7; selection/modify-line-{flex-column,flex-row,grid-basic}.tentative 2 each, [..]
- Fix: Bridge the two selections (bindings' Range vs browser/selection.cpp's visual selection) so modify() moves a caret position: map (node, offset) -> layout position via the existing caret hit-testing in browser/input.cpp, step by granularity (character = next grapheme in visual order; word = UAX#29 boundaries the engine's line breaker may already expose; line = same x on the adjacent line box; lineboundary/paragraphboundary = line box / block edges), clamp to the editing host when the focus is inside one, then write back with store_range. Start with character/word/lineboundary forward/backward in LTR (covers modify.tentative, modify-around-*, ~40 subtests), leave bidi 'left/right' and line movement for later. Whole-session work.

### focus() on a contenteditable host does not place the caret at its first caret position

- **149 subtests / 2 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: control_methods.cpp:108 `focus` only calls `on_focus_(id)` (browser focus + visual selection_focus_); the bindings' Selection (the Range in selection.cpp) is never collapsed into the editing host, so after `editor.focus()` the test's bracket-serialisation finds no caret ("Only one caret should be in the editor expected 1 but got 0", 99 of 100 subtests in each variant). Expected (Gecko/Blink, tentative): if the selection is not already inside the host, collapse it at the first caret position - (host,0) when empty, start of the first text node skipping collapsible leading whitespace (not under white-space: pre*), before the first <br>, before an empty inline, inside the first <p>.
- Spec: HTML §6.6.3 focusing steps / §6.6.5 focus update steps (editing host caret placement is UA-defined; tests are tentative and match Gecko+Blink)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/selection.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/selection.cpp
- Evidence: selection/contenteditable/initial-selection-on-focus.tentative.html?div 99/100 (+1 `addBrackets is not defined`: editing/include/tests.js is not in the fetched corpus); selection/contenteditable/initial-selection-on-focus.tentative.html?span 50/51
- Fix: In the focusing steps for an element that is an editing host (contenteditable attr, not inside another host): if the selection's range is not inside the host, compute the first caret position by a DOM walk (first text node -> offset past leading collapsible whitespace unless the computed white-space preserves it; first <br>/element -> (parent, index); nothing -> (host, 0)) and store_range a collapsed range there. Ties in with cluster 'selectionchange' (the change must queue the event). Also selection/contenteditable/collapse.html (3): Selection.collapse into an editing host should move focus (activeElement) to that host - the reverse direction, same hook.

### Request.formData()/Response missing (urlencoded body parsing surface)

- **70 subtests / 1 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: resources.cpp:296 defines `Request` and a fetch response object with only text()/json() (resources.cpp:452-455); there is no `Response` constructor ("Response is not defined" x35) and no `formData()` on either ("`formData` is undefined" x35). The test builds Request/Response with an application/x-www-form-urlencoded body and expects formData() to run the URL standard's urlencoded parser (the engine already has that parser for URLSearchParams).
- Spec: Fetch §5.5 Body mixin formData(); URL §5.1 application/x-www-form-urlencoded parser
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/resources.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/net/url.cpp
- Evidence: url/urlencoded-parser.any.js 70/105 (35 request.formData(), 35 response.formData())
- Fix: Add a `Response(body, init)` constructor sharing the fetch response prototype (status/ok/headers/text/json), and `formData()` on Request and Response: if Content-Type is application/x-www-form-urlencoded (charset parameter ignored, as Fetch says), run the urlencoded byte parser over the body bytes (UTF-8 decode with replacement) into a FormData; multipart can throw TypeError for now. Reuse URLSearchParams' parser in net/url.cpp.

### No selectionchange event is ever fired (document or text controls)

- **58 subtests / 7 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: rg selectionchange in lib/Shell finds only the onselectionchange attribute name (dispatch.cpp:1721) and the ponytail note in selection.cpp:25. Selection API §'scheduling a selectionchange event' and HTML's 'set the selection range' for input/textarea (control_methods.cpp:2965 set_selection) never queue a task; the four TIMEOUT files wait for the event forever and textcontrols/selectionchange.html counts 0 events after selectionStart=/selectionEnd=/setSelectionRange/select/setRangeText.
- Spec: Selection API §5 'scheduling a selectionchange event' (has scheduled selectionchange event flag); HTML §4.10.19.9 'set the selection range' step 5 (queue an element task to fire selectionchange, [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/selection.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/dispatch.cpp
- Evidence: selection/textcontrols/selectionchange.html 44/60 (expected 1 but got 0); selection/onselectionchange-on-document.html 4 TIMEOUT, selection/textcontrols/selectionchange-bubble.html 4 TIMEOUT, selection/textcontrols/onselectionchange-content-attribute.html 2 TIMEOUT; selection/onselectionchange-on-distinct-text-controls.html 2, selectionchange-on-shadow-dom.html 1, [..]
- Fix: store_range (selection.cpp:65) -> if the document's 'has scheduled selectionchange event' flag is false, set it and queue a task that clears it and fires `selectionchange` (bubbles=false, not cancelable) at the document. control_methods.cpp set_selection (and select/setRangeText/selectionStart/End setters) -> queue an element task to fire `selectionchange` at the control with bubbles=true (HTML 2023: it bubbles from text controls; selectionchange-bubble.html) - one flag per element so several writes in one task coalesce. Use the existing task queue the timers use (run_due_callbacks) so `await new Promise(setTimeout)` sees it; the onselectionchange content attribute on the body/document already resolves through the attribute list.

### Selection cannot point into a shadow tree; no composed-range rescoping

- **45 subtests / 10 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: selection.cpp:143-152 `rooted_here` requires root_of_tree(id) == the document root, so setBaseAndExtent/collapse/extend on a shadow-tree node silently abort (spec since 2023: the check is the SHADOW-INCLUDING root). When anchor and focus are in different trees the engine routes through Range.comparePoint, which throws WrongDocumentError (range.cpp:495 "the node is in another tree") - the spec sets start then end with 'set the start/end' which collapses instead, and keeps anchor/focus separately so getComposedRanges still answers. getComposedRanges (selection.cpp:362) ignores `options.shadowRoots`, never rescopes a boundary out of an unlisted shadow root to (host's parent, host index), and [..]
- Spec: Selection API §setBaseAndExtent / §extend (shadow-including root, 2023+); §getComposedRanges(options) rescoping; §containsNode; DOM §4.2.2 shadow-including root
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/selection.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/range.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/shadow_dom.cpp
- Evidence: selection/shadow-dom/tentative/Selection-getComposedRanges.html 7/12; selection/shadow-dom/tentative/Selection-getComposedRanges-dom-mutations-removal.html?mode=open 6/6 and ?mode=closed 6/6; selection/shadow-dom/tentative/Selection-getComposedRanges-slot.html 2, -collapsed.html 1, Selection-deleteFromDocument-around-shadow.html 2; [..]
- Fix: (1) rooted_here: walk shadow-including (root_of_tree then shadow host, repeat) and accept when it reaches this document. (2) setBaseAndExtent/extend: order the two points by a shadow-including compare (rescope both to the common tree first); if the trees differ, the live Range collapses at focus (spec 'set the start/end') but the selection remembers anchor+focus for getComposedRanges - store the four values in slots beside range_slot. (3) getComposedRanges(options): validate every entry is a ShadowRoot wrapper (TypeError), then for each boundary: while node's root is a shadow root not in the list, node = host, offset = index(host) (+1 for the end). (4) containsNode: false when the node is not in the flat tree (no assigned slot under a host). (5) On mutation (the live-range hook from c1110fac), if the selection's range root is no longer this document (host removed, range moved to a [..]

### Shared interface getters read a made document's node id from the primary document's tree (table.rows on createHTMLDocument)

- **42 subtests / 3 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence medium
- Root cause: Interface prototypes are shared across documents (second_document.cpp:27-35 adopt_interfaces_of) but the getters capture `this` = the primary bindings: control_methods.cpp:257 `table_rows` walks `doc_->read()` and the `rows` getter at :355 uses `receiver(c)` (wrapper.cpp:121 - unpacks the handle, no owner lookup). Only 12 call sites redirect through owner_of (reflection.cpp:1030 'THE DOCUMENT THAT OWNS THE RECEIVER answers'). Every template clearing-stack test does `doc = newHTMLDocument(); doc.body.innerHTML = '<table id=tbl><tbody><template>...</template><tr id=tr><td>...'; table.rows.length` and gets 0 - the parse is right (html5lib ?file=template passes 113/113; treebuilder.cpp:544 [..]
- Spec: HTML §4.9.1 HTMLTableElement.rows; DOM §4.5 document ownership (ownerDocument)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/control_methods.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/wrapper.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/second_document.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/reflection.cpp
- Evidence: html/syntax/parsing/template/clearing-the-stack-back-to-a-given-context/clearing-stack-back-to-a-table-body-context.html 33/33, ...-table-context.html 6/6, ...-table-row-context.html 3/3: all "Wrong number of table rows expected 1 but got 0"; probably also template/additions-to-the-in-{head,body}-insertion-mode/generating-of-implied-end-tags.html 2+2 ("Template element must be parsed got [..]
- Fix: Generalise reflection.cpp:1030's redirect into the installers: in `getter()`/`method()`/`operation()` (interfaces.cpp / control_methods.cpp) wrap each native so that `dom_bindings * owner = owner_of(c.current_this()); if (owner && owner != this) return owner->same_native(...)` - simplest is to install every prototype native as a thin trampoline that looks up the owner and calls the owner's copy of the lambda (store the lambdas in a table keyed by (interface, name) shared by all bindings). Or the cheaper first step: make `receiver()` return a (bindings*, node_id) and change the table/section/row getters (rows, tBodies, tHead, tFoot, caption, cells, sectionRowIndex, rowIndex) to read `owner->doc_`. Verify with a unit test: createHTMLDocument + innerHTML table + rows.length.

### JS strings are byte-indexed UTF-8 (the documented UTF-16 gap) - substring of a non-ASCII char returns its first byte

- **15 subtests / 1 files**, size L, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: docs/script.md 'The UTF-16 gap': string_object holds std::string UTF-8 and every index method is byte-based (string.cpp:551-563 substring, :324 charAt, :461 charCodeAt use s.size()). with-inheritance.html does `content.substring(i, i+1)` on a decoded windows-1253 ζ and gets the byte 0xCE ("expected 'ζ' but got 'Î'", 15 subtests) - the decode is right, the string model is wrong. VM-owned; listed here because it is the whole remainder of that file.
- Spec: ECMA-262 §6.1.4 String type (UTF-16 code units)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/text/string.cpp; ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/script/value.hpp; ctbrowser/docs/script.md
- Evidence: html/syntax/charset/with-inheritance.html: 15 'Check character' subtests (expected ζ or ж, got the first UTF-8 byte)
- Fix: Owned by the VM/test262 agents: either a UTF-16 (or latin1/UTF-16 dual) string_object, or, as a bridge, code-unit-indexed accessors (length/charAt/charCodeAt/substring/slice/indexOf) that map UTF-16 indices to byte offsets with a cached ASCII-only flag so the common case stays O(1). unittests/js/string_basics.cpp pins today's wrong answers as the acceptance list.

### Encoding: no 'change the encoding' after the 1024-byte prescan, and the prescan sees <meta> inside script/style/title

- **13 subtests / 3 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: (a) treebuilder.cpp has no encoding hook (rg encoding: only <annotation-xml encoding>), so a <meta charset> found by the tree builder after the prescan window never triggers HTML 13.2.3.4 'changing the encoding while parsing' - after-1kb.html, after-head-in-1kb(-crlf).html and ncr.html keep the parent's windows-1253 instead of the declared windows-1251. (b) encoding.cpp:428 prescan_encoding treats `<script><meta charset=windows-1251></script>` (and <style>, <title>) as a real <meta> tag, so in-script/in-style/in-title switch to 1251 when the reftests (rel=mismatch) expect the inherited 1253. (c) inheritance-bogus-meta(-utf-8): a child with an unrecognised <meta charset> label must inherit [..]
- Spec: HTML §13.2.3.2 determining the character encoding step 8 (parent inheritance); §13.2.3.3 prescan a byte stream; §13.2.3.4 changing the encoding while parsing; §13.2.6.4.4 in head <meta>
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/DOM/encoding.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/DOM/treebuilder.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp
- Evidence: html/syntax/charset/with-inheritance.html: 7 'Check characterSet' subtests (after-1kb, after-head-in-1kb, after-head-in-1kb-crlf, ncr expect windows-1251; in-script, in-style, in-title expect windows-1253); html/syntax/charset/inheritance-bogus-meta.html 3/4 (TIMEOUT), inheritance-bogus-meta-utf-8.html 3/3
- Fix: (a) In the tree builder's in-head <meta> step, when the document's encoding confidence is 'tentative' (inherited/default) and the token declares a supported charset different from the current one, stop and re-run the parse of the same bytes with the new encoding (the loader has the bytes; frames.cpp:294-299 already re-decodes) - the navigate-again path, cheap here since the whole byte stream is in memory. (b) In prescan_encoding, when a tag start is script/style/title/textarea/xmp/noembed/noframes/iframe/noscript, skip to the matching case-insensitive `</name` before continuing (what a real tokenizer sees; matches the reftests). (c) An unknown label in the child -> keep inheriting; check decode of the utf-8 parent + bogus child gives U+FFFD for invalid bytes.

### insertAdjacentHTML on a parentless element / Document parent throws the wrong thing or nothing

- **13 subtests / 2 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: node_methods.cpp:163-200 adjacent_place: a parentless element returns nullopt silently (spec: throw NoModificationAllowedError) and an element whose parent is the Document throws HierarchyRequestError (spec: NoModificationAllowedError). The comment cites the OLD spec text ('return null'); DOM Parsing/HTML now says step 2 'If context is null or a Document, throw NoModificationAllowedError'.
- Spec: HTML §insertAdjacentHTML(position, string) step 2 (formerly DOM Parsing §3.2)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element/node_methods.cpp
- Evidence: domparsing/insert_adjacent_html.html 6/31 (3 'parent node is null ... did not throw', 3 'parent is a document ... threw HierarchyRequestError'); domparsing/insert_adjacent_html-xhtml.xhtml 7/30 (same)
- Fix: In adjacent_place for beforebegin/afterend: if parent is null or a Document node -> throw_dom_exception("NoModificationAllowedError"). Keep the SyntaxError for bad positions. One-line change plus the comment.

### Customizable <select>: <selectedcontent>/<button> inside <select> parsed by the old 'in select' rules

- **12 subtests / 3 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence medium
- Root cause: treebuilder.cpp has no `selectedcontent` (rg: nothing) and its 'in select' mode is the pre-2025 one; webkit02.dat's `<select><button><selectedcontent></button><option>X` cases expect the customizable-select tree (button and selectedcontent kept as children of select, option after the button).
- Spec: HTML §13.2.6.4.16 'in select' insertion mode (current text); HTML §4.10.7 the select element, selectedcontent
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/DOM/treebuilder.cpp
- Evidence: html/syntax/parsing/html5lib_url.html?file=webkit02 4/48, html5lib_write.html?file=webkit02 4/44, html5lib_write_single.html?file=webkit02 4/44 - all the <select><button><selectedcontent> cases
- Fix: Port the current spec's 'in select' insertion mode (the 2025 customizable select change: start tags button/selectedcontent/div etc. inside select are inserted rather than ignored; <option>/<optgroup>/<hr> keep their pops; <select> inside <select> pops) and add `selectedcontent` to the tag tables. Re-run the whole html5lib set for regressions (1,723 fixtures pass today).

### Engine-fired load/DOMContentLoaded/pageshow have the wrong flags and pageshow never fires

- **5 subtests / 2 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: dispatch.cpp:682 make_event_object(cx, type, true, true) makes every engine event bubbles=true, cancelable=true; the-end.html asserts DOMContentLoaded cancelable=false ("cancelable should be false expected false got true"), load bubbles=false with target=document, and a pageshow (PageTransitionEvent, bubbles+cancelable, persisted=false) after load - never dispatched (rg pageshow: nothing), so 'load'/'pageshow'/'order' time out. DOMContentLoaded-defer.html: DOMContentLoaded fires before a task queued by a defer script (frame.cpp:111 fires it synchronously after parse, before run_due_callbacks).
- Spec: HTML §13.2.7 'the end' steps 3-6; HTML §7.4.6 completely finish loading (pageshow); PageTransitionEvent
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/frame.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/dispatch.cpp
- Evidence: html/syntax/parsing/the-end.html 4/4 (1 FAIL + 3 TIMEOUT); html/syntax/parsing/DOMContentLoaded-defer.html 1/1
- Fix: Give the engine-event maker per-type flags: DOMContentLoaded {bubbles:true, cancelable:false}, load {false,false} targeted at the document but dispatched at the window; fire pageshow (PageTransitionEvent with persisted=false) right after load (HTML 7.4.6 'completely finish loading' step). Run defer scripts and then DOMContentLoaded as tasks in order (HTML 13.2.7 'the end' steps 3-4: 'spin the event loop until scripts that will execute when the document has finished parsing is empty', then queue a global task for DOMContentLoaded).

### The page parser never attaches declarative shadow roots (<template shadowrootmode> in loaded markup)

- **3 subtests / 5 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: attach_declarative_shadow_roots (shadow_dom.cpp:231) is called only from setHTMLUnsafe (shadow_dom.cpp:342) and the parseHTMLUnsafe/DOMParser path (:366). treebuilder.cpp has no shadow attachment (rg shadow: nothing), and neither browser/frame load nor frames.cpp calls the helper, so in a normally loaded page `host.shadowRoot` is null and `root.getElementById(...)` throws before any test registers (HARNESS_ERROR). document.hpp:262's 'THE PARSER ATTACHED THIS ONE' flag exists for exactly this and is set only by the two fragment paths.
- Spec: HTML §13.2.6.4.9 'in head' end tag template: declarative shadow root attachment steps; HTML §4.12.2 template shadowrootmode
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/shadow_dom.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/frame.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/frames.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/DOM/treebuilder.cpp
- Evidence: HARNESS_ERROR: selection/shadow-dom/tentative/Selection-isCollapsed.html, selection/shadow-dom/tentative/Selection-getComposedRanges-range-update.html ("Cannot read properties of null (reading 'getElementById')"), selection/shadow-dom/cross-shadow-boundary-extend.html ("reading 'querySelector'"); selection/shadow-dom/tentative/Range-isPointInRange.html 2/2 (same null shadowRoot); [..]
- Fix: After the page (and each frame document, frames.cpp ~line 300) finishes parsing, call attach_declarative_shadow_roots(*doc_, document root) exactly as setHTMLUnsafe does - or better, do it from the tree builder's template end-tag step (HTML 13.2.6.4.9 'in template' </template>: if the template has shadowrootmode and its parent can host, attach and move the contents) so document.write and streaming parses get it too. Hits the shadow-dom suite (other slice) at least as hard - four files here hide whole test files.

- Spec: CSSOM View §14 'update the rendering' / forced layout; Media Queries 4 §4.1 width/height are the viewport's; CSS Values 4 §6.1.2 viewport-percentage lengths (the frame's viewport); HTML 4.8.5 iframe [..]

- Fix: (1) Make the forced-layout entry the bindings use for getBoundingClientRect/getComputedStyle/matchMedia (the `ensure_layout` path in frame.cpp) run `layout_frames()` whenever frames_stale() - and mark frames stale synchronously when a frame element's style/width/height attribute changes, not on the next tick. CSS Values 5 §14: tree counting functions are resolved at computed-value time against the element the value applies to - a keyframe's [..]
- Spec: CSS Values 5 §14 tree counting functions (computed-value time, on the element); CSS Animations 1 §4 (keyframe values computed on the target); CSS Properties & Values 1 §3.2 registered property [..]
- Engine files: /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/animations/css.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/substitute.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/keyframes.cpp
