- Fix: When an animation's keyframes are computed for a target (the point where custom properties and var() are substituted into keyframe values - bindings/animations/css.cpp around 420-440), run the same per-element fold the cascade runs for a declared value: expose `engine::compute_value_for(node, property, text)` (substitute -> tree-counting facts -> math fold -> registered-property syntax check) and use it for every keyframe value, re-running when the target's sibling facts change (the '-dynamic' files remove #rm and expect the value to follow: the style-change event must invalidate running animations' computed keyframes). Allow the functions in @container size/style queries (media.cpp's `feature` gets the facts from chain_styles_) and refuse them in descriptors (parser.cpp font-face/counter-style blocks).

### var() is not yet an arbitrary substitution function: name from a substitution, any <declaration-value> in the arguments, CSS-wide keywords as fallback (51 subtests, 9 files)

- **51 subtests / 9 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: lib/Style/css/substitute.cpp validates var() at parse time against the Variables 1 grammar `var( <custom-property-name> , <declaration-value>? )`: `var(var(--myvar))` (name produced by an inner substitution), `var(--x ())`, `var({--x})` and `var(prop)` are refused at parse time (el.style stores '' - variable-reference.html expects the specified text to round-trip, var-parsing.html expects the property to be set), while css-variables-2 §2.2/§3.1 make the first argument a <declaration-value> that is substituted first and then must parse as a <custom-property-name> (guaranteed-invalid -> fallback, 'invalid substituted name falls back' expected 20px got 1008px). A CSS-wide keyword produced as [..]
- Spec: CSS Variables 2 §2.2 var() syntax (<declaration-value> name), §3.1 'replace a var() function'; CSS Values 5 §2.2 arbitrary substitution functions (CSS-wide keyword result); CSS Values 5 §2.3 attr() [..]
- Engine files: /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/substitute.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/style/css/substitute.hpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/declaration.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp
- Evidence: css/css-variables/variable-reference-name-substitution.html 13/24 ('invalid substituted name falls back' expected "20px" got "1008px"; 'unset name-providing var() falls back'); css/css-variables/var-parsing.html 8/18 ("var(--x ())" should set the property value), variable-reference.html 8/18 (width: var(prop) expected "var(prop)" got ""); css/css-variables/variable-css-wide-keywords.html 7/30 [..]
- Fix: Parse-time (declaration.cpp value_check + substitute.cpp's may_substitute scan): a var() whose first argument is a balanced <declaration-value> and whose optional second part follows a top-level comma is VALID at parse time (store the text as written; CSSOM serializes it back). Computed-value time (substituter::run / the var arm): substitute the name argument first (recursively, depth-limited), trim, require a single ident token starting with `--` (else guaranteed-invalid -> use fallback or the whole declaration invalid at computed-value time), then look it up; carry attr-taint through the name. After substitution of the whole value, if the result is exactly one CSS-wide keyword (initial/inherit/unset/revert/revert-layer/revert-rule), hand it to the cascade's keyword resolver (engine.cpp:1300-1345 `put`) instead of the grammar check. Unit tests are the four test names above.

SVG presentation attributes (`stroke-width="var(--stroke1)"`, fill, stroke, opacity...) are mapped nowhere (rg '"stroke-width"' finds [..]

- Engine files: /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/layout/box.hpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/style/engine.hpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table.cpp

- Fix: Give the engine a per-element presentational-hint source the way the style attribute already is: in engine.cpp's match collection, before the author sheets, synthesize declarations from HTML's mapped attributes (img/iframe/video/table/td width,height -> px or %; bgcolor, align, hidden...) and the SVG presentation attribute list (SVG 2 'presentation attributes': every property in the table whose name matches an attribute on an SVG-namespace element), tagged origin=author, layer index below every real layer, specificity 0, so `revert` strikes them (origin == author) and `revert-layer` strikes only the layers above them (matches_.origin/layer are what rolled_back keys on, engine.cpp:1310-1340). Values go through the same var()/substitute pass as any declaration. Then delete the box.hpp attribute fallbacks (they become dead: keep `<table border>` which is paint). - Spec: CSS Values 5 §10.1 calc-size() (size in the box-sizing box); CSS Sizing 3 §5.1; CSS Flexbox 1 §9.2.3 flex base size
- Engine files: /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Layout/flex.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Layout/algorithm.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/layout/algorithm.hpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/grammar.cpp

- Fix: Read the three tests' fixtures (they state the content sizes) and trace calc_over_content's `content` argument for a column flex item and for a block with padding: make `size` the basis in the box-sizing box (content box unless border-box), subtract the inner edges exactly once, and for a column container measure the BLOCK intrinsic size (lay the child out at the cross size, take its auto height) rather than the inline measure. Accept `stretch` as a calc-size basis in grammar.cpp. Net: css/css-flexbox + css/css-sizing.

### CSS Nesting parser/serializer fidelity: a nested rule starting with a combinator is dropped, `&` is canonicalised instead of kept as written, bare declarations after a nested group rule vanish (32 subtests, 10 files; also explains at-scope-relative-syntax's 8)

- **32 subtests / 10 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: lib/Style/css/parser.cpp's style-rule body reader (lines 289-470) decides declaration-vs-rule per CSS Syntax 3 §5.4.4 but a prelude beginning with `>`, `+`, `~` is not recognised as a rule (parsing.html: '.foo { > .bar { } }' Inner rule should exist expected 1 got 0 - CSS Nesting 1 §2.1 allows a <relative-selector-list>, the same anchoring selector.cpp:806-818 already does for :has()). selectorText is regenerated from the compiled selector (`.class&` -> `&.class`, `&&` -> `&`, `:is(.bar,.baz)` loses the implied `& `), where CSSOM serializes the selector as parsed with `&` in place. Declarations that follow a nested rule inside a nested @media are dropped ('Implicit like rule not in first [..]
- Spec: CSS Nesting 1 §2.1 (relative selectors, &), §2.3 (nested group rules), §3 CSSNestedDeclarations; CSSOM §6.4.3 selectorText serialization
- Engine files: /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/parser.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/selector.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/stylesheets/rules.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/stylesheets/serialize.cpp
- Evidence: css/css-nesting/parsing.html 11/32 ('.foo { > .bar { color: green; }}' Inner rule should exist expected 1 got 0; '.class&' expected ".class&" got "&.class"); css/css-nesting/serialize-group-rules-with-decls.html 6/15, nested-declarations-cssom.html 3/12, cssom.html 2/14, invalid-inner-rules.html 2/2 (@font-face inside a style rule must be rejected), nested-declarations-cssom-whitespace 2/2, [..]
- Fix: (1) In the body reader: a run that starts with a combinator token (`>`, `+`, `~`) or with `&`/ident/`.`/`#`/`[`/`:`/`*` followed by a `{` block at depth 0 is a nested rule; compile its prelude as a relative selector list anchored on `&` (selector.cpp's :has() relative parse, with the nesting parent instead of :scope). (2) Keep the selector's source text (whitespace-collapsed) on the rule record for selectorText/cssText; the compiled form stays for matching. (3) Every maximal run of declarations that is not the first in a nested block (style rule or nested group rule) files a CSSNestedDeclarations record with the parent's selector, in source order (§3.1); an empty one serializes nothing. (4) Reject `&` immediately followed by a type selector and non-nestable at-rules (@font-face, @keyframes...) inside style rules. Net: css/css-nesting + css/css-cascade/scope-*.

- Evidence: css/css-syntax/serialize-consecutive-tokens.html 16/72; css/css-variables/variable-substitution-basic.html 3/13 ('Multiple variable references in a single property (no spaces)' expected "23px 59px" got "23px59px"; "You can't build up a single token where part of it is provided by a var" expected 0px got 20px)
- Fix: Replace join()'s name-char heuristic with the §9.1 table keyed on the LAST token type of `left` and the FIRST token type of `right` (tokenize each side's boundary: ident/function/url/bad-url/'-'/number/percentage/dimension/CDC/'('/'*'/'%'/'@'/'#'): emit `/**/` for every marked pair; and re-tokenize the substituted value once at the end so `2` + `3px` stays two tokens (the whole-value re-tokenisation css-variables-1 §3 describes). ### Stylesheet byte decoding ignores @charset / BOM / HTTP charset / the referring document's encoding (15 files)

- **15 subtests / 15 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: css/css-syntax/charset/*: a windows-1251 page loading a sheet whose selector is `U+0418` fails 15 of 16 files ('expected hidden got visible') - the fetched CSS bytes are handed to the parser as UTF-8 regardless of the BOM, an `@charset "..."` rule, the HTTP Content-Type charset, the `<link charset>` attribute or the referring document's encoding (CSS Syntax 3 §3.2 'determine the fallback encoding' and 'decode bytes'). wpt-next.md lines 356-357 already name this ('css-syntax 15/40: @charset and byte decoding'). The Encoding Standard label table exists in the DOM half (document.characterSet work, round W).
- Spec: CSS Syntax 3 §3.2 'decode bytes' and 'determine the fallback encoding'; Encoding Standard §4.2 'get an encoding'
- Engine files: /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/resources.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Shell/browser/styles.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/resources.cpp
- Evidence: css/css-syntax/charset/page-windows-1251-css-no-decl.html 1/1, page-windows-1251-css-utf8-bom.html, page-windows-1251-css-at-charset-utf16(be).html, page-windows-1251-css-http-bogus.html, ...-charset-attribute-bogus.html, xml-stylesheet-page-windows-1251-charset-attribute-windows-1250.xhtml - 15 files x 1 subtest 'expected "hidden" but got "visible"'
- Fix: In the stylesheet fetch completion: decode with (1) BOM, else (2) the HTTP charset label, else (3) `@charset "x";` in the first 1024 bytes (exact byte pattern, utf-16 labels map to utf-8), else (4) the <link charset> attribute, else (5) the referring document's encoding, else utf-8 - then feed the decoded text to the parser. Reuse the DOM's Encoding-Standard label table and windows-125x decoders (lib/DOM's meta-charset work); constructed sheets and <style> are already text and unaffected.

### Level-4 text/inline/multicol/overflow properties and values absent from the property table (or typed as a scalar kind that refuses their multi-value grammar)

- **668 subtests / 51 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: find_property returns nullptr (so CSS.supports is false and getComputedStyle does not publish) for text-spacing, text-fit, text-group-align, word-space-transform, column-wrap, scroll-axis-lock, scroll-buttons/scroll-markers (css-overflow-5), `size` (css-sizing-4 shorthand), and the display values grid-lanes/inline-grid-lanes; and rows that exist have a scalar kind that refuses the Level-4 grammar: text-indent is k::length_percentage so `0px each-line` / `50px hanging` is refused (table.cpp:244), hyphenate-limit-chars is k::integer so `5 1 2` and `auto 1 2` are refused (table.cpp:305), initial-letter's `<number> <integer>?` (grammar.cpp integer_slots_ok only), line-clamp/max-lines, [..]
- Spec: CSS Text 4 §2.2 (text-indent hanging/each-line), §5.3 (hyphenate-limit-chars), §8.3 text-spacing, §3 white-space longhands; CSS Inline 3 §3.1 initial-letter, §5 vertical-align; CSS Box 4 §3.4 [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table.cpp (text-indent 244, hyphenate-limit-chars 305, vertical-align 270); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table_modules.cpp (text-wrap 228-233, margin-trim, overflow-clip-margin, display list); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/keywords.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/grammar.cpp (integer_slots_ok 389-433); [..]
- Evidence: css/css-text/animations/text-indent-interpolation.html 132 + text-indent-composition 24 ('from' value should be supported: `[0px each-line]`, `[50px hanging]`); css/css-text/animations/hyphen-limit-chars-interpolation.html 64 (`[5 1 2]`, `[auto 1 2]`) + parsing/hyphenate-limit-chars-{computed,valid} 9+9; css/css-inline/animation/initial-letter-no-interpolation.html 42, [..]
- Fix: Table rows first (each is one line in table_modules.cpp with the spec's keywords/initial/inherited): text-spacing (`normal | none | auto | [trim-start||space-first||...]`), text-fit, text-group-align, word-space-transform, column-wrap, scroll-axis-lock, `size` as a shorthand of width/height, display's grid-lanes pair. Then the grammars that a scalar kind cannot express: make text-indent freeform with a small matcher `<length-percentage> && hanging? && each-line?` (canonical order: length, hanging, each-line), hyphenate-limit-chars `[auto | <integer>]{1,3}` (computed keeps `auto` for the omitted slots: `5` -> `5 auto`), initial-letter `normal | <number> [<integer> | drop | raise]?`, vertical-align per css-inline-3 §5 (`0` and `baseline` fold to `baseline`), margin-trim (`none | block | inline | [block-start||inline-start||block-end||inline-end]` with the pair-to-axis fold and duplicate [..]

### System font keywords (`font: caption | icon | menu | message-box | small-caption | status-bar`) are kept as an opaque whole and set no longhand

- **366 subtests / 2 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: split_font (shorthands.cpp:378-385) returns split::whole for a single system-font keyword, so the cascade stores `font: caption` and font-style/variant/weight/stretch/size/line-height/family keep their previous values; getComputedStyle's font fold answers "" and an animation from an explicit font to `caption` interpolates each longhand against the OLD value (weight 400, size 16px, stretch 100%).
- Spec: CSS Fonts 4 §3.1.1 (system fonts: 'must be treated as a shorthand... set all the subproperties')
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/shorthands.cpp (split_font 374-430, the `system` keyword set 383-385); ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp (font pre-pass 1632-1699 reads the size a `font` carries)
- Evidence: css/css-fonts/animations/system-fonts.html 360/588 (`property <font> from [italic 100 small-caps ultra-expanded 100px / 100px Ahem] to [caption] at (0) :: expected "100 " but got "400 "`, `expected "100px " but got "16px "`, `expected "small-caps" but got "normal"`); css/css-fonts/parsing/font-computed.html 6 (`caption should be a supported system font. :: font should be set got disallowed value [..]
- Fix: In split_font, when the single word is a system keyword, emit the seven longhands from a fixed UA table instead of split::whole: style normal, variant normal, weight 400, stretch 100%, size 13.3333px (caption/icon/menu/message-box/status-bar) or 11px (small-caption), line-height normal, family sans-serif (any consistent choice passes: the harness derives its expectation from getComputedStyle of the same engine). Keep the read-back rule that a `font` whose longhands equal a system entry may serialise as the keyword only for el.style, not for the computed style (CSS Fonts 4 §3.1: the computed style is the longhands).

### `inherit` in a keyframe stays the literal word: the sampler has no way to ask for the parent's computed value

- **346 subtests / 33 files**, size S, overlaps round7, confidence high
- Root cause: animations.cpp:359-372 resolves `initial` and non-inherited `unset` to the table's initial (round seven) but carries an explicit ponytail note: "`inherit` needs the parent's computed text, which the underlying callback cannot ask for yet". The `underlying` callback built at entries.cpp:324 only reads `declared_on(at.chain.front(), property)`; the parent is `at.chain[1]` one line away. A keyframe `from { widows: inherit }` therefore emits "inherit" (or, for an inherited property with `unset`, the wrong value) and every pair with it is non-interpolable.
- Spec: CSS Animations 1 §3 ('the values of the keyframes... are computed values'); Web Animations 1 §5.4.3; CSS Cascade 4 §7.3 (inherit/unset)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/animations.cpp (359-372, 381-386); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/computed_style/entries.cpp (323-327 the underlying callback); ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/shell/bindings.hpp (animated_values signature)
- Evidence: 33 animation files, 14 subtests each in e.g. css/css-break/animation/widows-interpolation.html (`CSS Animations: property <widows> from [inherit] to [20] at (-3) :: expected "60" but got "inherit"`), css/css-fonts/animations/font-{palette,stretch,style}-interpolation.html, css/css-multicol/animation/column-{width,count}-interpolation.html (`from [inherit] to [20] at (0) :: expected "30" but got [..]
- Fix: Give animated_values a second callback `parent(property)` (entries.cpp passes `declared_on(at.chain.size() >= 2 ? at.chain[1] : node_id{}, property)`, css.cpp's before-change sampler passes the parent's previous style) and in the keyframe loop map `inherit`, and `unset` when known->inherited, to `parent(property)` falling back to known->initial when empty. Same three lines make Web Animations' `{width: 'inherit'}` right. Also apply the mapping AFTER shorthand expansion (css.cpp:633-650 expands `margin: initial` into longhands - check each longhand's keyword hits this branch).

### `grid` and `grid-template` have no shorthand split or fold: stored whole, longhands empty, invalid forms accepted

- **337 subtests / 11 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: shorthands.cpp's shape table (120-160) has grid-row/grid-column/grid-area (shape::grid_lines) but nothing for `grid` or `grid-template`; table_modules.cpp:72-73 marks both `shorthand` so getComputedStyle enumerates them, and check_declaration reaches match_grid only for `grid-*` longhands (declaration.cpp:385, match_grid returns false for the shorthands), so `grid: none none`, `grid-template: 10px`, `grid: 20%` are stored as valid, `grid: none` sets no grid-auto-* / grid-template-areas longhand, and the read-back is the author's bytes (`auto-flow auto / 100px 100px`) instead of the canonical shortest form (`none / 100px 100px`).
- Spec: CSS Grid 2 §7.4 (grid-template), §7.8 (grid), §7.2/7.3; CSSOM §6.7.2 serialize a CSS declaration block (shorthand shortest serialization)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/shorthands.cpp (shape table 120-160, split dispatch 640-680, fold dispatch 800-830); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/grid.cpp (track_list 417, template_areas 452, line_names 208 - the pieces a grid-template split reuses); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table_modules.cpp (72-73)
- Evidence: css/css-grid/parsing/grid-shorthand-serialization.html 89/141 (`grid: auto-flow auto / 100px 100px should set grid :: expected "none / 100px 100px" but got "auto-flow auto / 100px 100px"`, `should set grid-auto-flow :: expected "row" but got ""`); css/css-grid/parsing/grid-template-shorthand-invalid.html 66/66 (`"auto" should not set the property value`, `"none none"`, `"10px"`), [..]
- Fix: Add shape::grid_template and shape::grid to shorthands.cpp. split_grid_template: `none` -> rows none / columns none / areas none; `<rows> / <columns>` via grid.cpp track_list twice; the areas form `[<line-names>? <string> <track-size>? <line-names>?]+ [/ <explicit-track-list>]?` -> rows = the sizes (auto when omitted) with merged line names, areas = the strings, columns = the explicit list or none. split_grid: `<'grid-template'>` (auto-* reset to auto/row) | `<rows> / [auto-flow && dense?] <'grid-auto-columns'>?` | `[auto-flow && dense?] <'grid-auto-rows'>? / <columns>`; refuse anything else. fold_grid_template/fold_grid produce the shortest form (CSS Grid 2 §7.4/§7.8 + CSSOM §6.7.2: `none` when everything is initial, `none / 100px 100px`, "" when the longhands cannot be expressed - e.g. grid-auto-flow column with explicit rows AND columns). Wire computed read-back through the existing [..]

### Keyword computed values are not absolutised at cascade time: font-weight normal/bold, font-stretch/font-width keywords, font-style oblique angles, word-/letter-spacing normal, column-rule-width thin/medium/thick

- **319 subtests / 11 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: The cascade stores these keywords as text (table.cpp:214 font-weight k::number keywords 'normal bold bolder lighter' with no mapping, 216 font-stretch freeform, 240-241 letter/word-spacing 'normal', table_modules.cpp:101 column-rule-width 'thin medium thick') and neither engine.cpp's pass two nor entries.cpp maps them, so getComputedStyle answers `normal`/`condensed`/`medium` where the spec's computed value is `400`/`75%`/`3px`, and easing.cpp's numeric_of sees a keyword on one side and declares the pair discrete: `font-stretch` [condensed]->[expanded] flips, `[normal]`->`[oblique 10deg]` flips, transitions from `word-spacing: initial` (`normal`) never start, `column-rule-width: initial` [..]

- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp (pass two 2043-2100, beside the font-size pre-pass 1632-1699); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table.cpp (214-226, 240-241); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table_modules.cpp (101); ctbrowser/lib/Style/easing.cpp (computed_shape 417, numeric_of, numeric_text 257-279); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/computed_style/entries.cpp (900-940 the length fold)

- Fix: One table-driven fold in engine.cpp pass two (where the relative lengths already fold to px): font-weight normal->400, bold->700, bolder/lighter relative to the parent's computed weight per CSS Fonts 4 §2.2's table, clamp [1,1000]; font-stretch/font-width ultra-condensed 50%, extra-condensed 62.5%, condensed 75%, semi-condensed 87.5%, normal 100%, semi-expanded 112.5%, expanded 125%, extra-expanded 150%, ultra-expanded 200% (reverse map only in the `font` shorthand fold, which 7a4c7feb already does); font-style `oblique` -> `oblique 14deg`, `oblique 0deg` -> `normal`, angle to deg and clamp ±90; letter-spacing/word-spacing normal -> 0px; the line-width keywords thin/medium/thick -> 1px/3px/5px for column-rule-width, outline-width and border-*-width (check which of those entries.cpp already maps). Mark font-stretch nonnegative. easing.cpp then interpolates them as [..]

### grid-template-columns/rows have no interpolation shape: track lists animate discretely and composition ignores the underlying list

- **268 subtests / 4 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: easing.cpp's computed_shape/interpolate_pair (417, 811, 1104) know numerics, colours, transforms, filters, shadows and lists of those; there is no track-list shape and no `fr` unit anywhere in the file (rg 'fr\b' matches nothing). CSS Grid 2 says a track list animates by computed value when both lists have the same length and the same line names and every track size pair is numeric (length-percentage or flex), else discrete. So `1fr 1fr 1fr` -> `2fr auto 2fr` is a flip (expected `1.4fr 1fr 1.4fr` at 0.4: per-track, with `1fr`->`auto` discrete at 50%), and composition add of `50px 10%` + `100px 100px` gives `150px calc(10% + 100px)`.
- Spec: CSS Grid 2 §7.2 grid-template-columns 'Animation type: if the list lengths match, by computed value; otherwise discrete' (and the line-names/repeat() conditions in the same section); CSS Values 4 [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/easing.cpp (computed_shape 417-520, interpolate_pair 811/1104, add_pair 1211, numeric_of 192-230, numeric_text 257-279); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/grid.cpp (track_list, line_names - reuse to tokenise the list into names and sizes)
- Evidence: css/css-grid/animation/grid-template-columns-interpolation.html 46 (`from [1fr 1fr 1fr] to [2fr auto 2fr] at (0.4) :: expected "1.4fr 1fr 1.4fr" but got "2fr auto 2fr"`; `from [1fr [a b] 1fr [d] 1fr] to [2fr [c] auto 2fr] at (0.6) :: expected "1.6fr [c] auto 1.6fr"`) + 396 more once CSS.supports accepts repeat()/minmax(); css/css-grid/animation/grid-template-rows-interpolation.html 46 (+396); [..]
- Fix: Add a track_list shape in easing.cpp: split each side with grid.cpp's cursor into a vector of {line-names, size}; interpolable iff same length, identical name lists, and each size pair is either both `<length-percentage>` (numeric_of), both `<flex>` (treat `fr` as a third numeric type, serialise `Nfr`), or both minmax()/fit-content() with pairwise-interpolable arguments; repeat(N, ...) only against repeat(N, ...) with equal N; anything else discrete (flip at 50%). Emit numeric_text per track with grid's canonical spacing. add_pair for the same shape sums track by track (percent mix as calc). Depends on the value_functions fix for the repeat()/minmax() cases.

### Resolved value of grid-template-* on a grid container must be the used track list; there is no grid track-sizing algorithm to produce one

- **159 subtests / 8 files**, size L, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: CSSOM's resolved-value special case: for a grid container, getComputedStyle(grid-template-columns) is the used value - every track in px, repeat() expanded (auto-fill/auto-fit counted against the container width), implicit tracks included, percentages and minmax()/auto/min-content resolved. grid.cpp:626-641 computed_grid absolutises lengths only (the commit 6470eba3 says 'a grid container's used track sizes need a layout this engine does not do') and entries.cpp:131-134/304-309 records that `display: grid` lays out as a block with no grid box kind. So `none` on a container with content reads `none` instead of `300px`, `20%` reads `20%` instead of `60px`, `minmax(min-content, 15px) auto` [..]
- Spec: CSS Grid 2 §12 (grid sizing algorithm), §7.2.3.2 (repeat-to-fill), §8.1 implicit grid; CSSOM §resolved-value ('grid-template-columns/rows: the resolved value is the used value' when the element [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Layout/** (no grid algorithm exists; box_builder/algorithm.cpp lay `display: grid` out as block); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/grid.cpp (computed_grid 626-641); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/computed_style/entries.cpp (grid_property 959-1040, at.grid_item 304-309)
- Evidence: css/css-grid/parsing/grid-content-sized-columns-resolution.html 42/42 (`Element 'gridMinContentFixedAndAuto' grid-template-columns value computes to '15px 75px' :: got "minmax(min-content, 15px) auto"`); css/css-grid/parsing/grid-columns-rows-get-set-multiple.html 27/40 (`expected "400px 800px" but got "50% 100%"`); css/css-grid/parsing/grid-template-{columns,rows}-computed-implicit-track.html [..]
- Fix: This is the grid layout algorithm (CSS Grid 2 §12: initialise track sizes, resolve intrinsic sizes from items' min/max-content contributions, maximise, expand flexible tracks, stretch auto) plus §7.2.3 auto-repeat counting and §8.1 implicit track creation, then entries.cpp asking the box for its used tracks. Not a parsing fix; belongs to whoever builds grid layout, and the reftests that would measure it are not run (run-wpt.py skips rel=match). Until then these 159 stay red; the parsing-only wins are clusters 1, 6 and 8.

### Font property grammars kept freeform: font-variant shorthand, font-variant-alternates, font-variation-settings, font-feature-settings, font-synthesis, font-family/font invalid forms

- **148 subtests / 18 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: table.cpp:213-226 and table_modules.cpp:205-218 type these as k::freeform with no matcher in keywords.cpp/grammar.cpp, so any token sequence is accepted and serialised as written: `font-variant: normal none`/`small-caps normal` (normal/none are exclusive), `swash(several,different,aliases)` (one ident), `font-variation-settings: 700` / `wght 700` (must be `<string> <number>`), `font-family` with a bare number, `font: ...` invalid forms; and font-synthesis (a shorthand of the four -weight/-style/-small-caps/-position longhands, table_modules.cpp:208) has no split/fold so its computed value is "" and `oblique-only` is refused.
- Spec: CSS Fonts 4 §2.1 font-family, §3.1 font, §6.1 font-variant, §6.8 font-variant-alternates, §7.1 font-feature-settings, §11.1 font-variation-settings, §2.7 font-synthesis, §2.6 font-size-adjust
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table.cpp (213-226); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table_modules.cpp (205-218); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/keywords.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/grammar.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/shorthands.cpp (font-synthesis split/fold; font-variant split); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/font_family.cpp
- Evidence: css/css-fonts/parsing/font-synthesis-computed.html 21/21 (`'none' :: expected "none" but got ""`), font-synthesis-valid 9/23; css/css-fonts/parsing/font-variant-invalid.html 21/21 (`"normal none" should not set the property value`), font-variant-alternates-invalid 15/15, font-variant-{valid,serialization} 2+1; css/css-fonts/parsing/font-variation-settings-invalid.html 18/18 (`"700"`, `"wght [..]
- Fix: One matcher per grammar, wired like match_alignment in check_declaration: font-variation-settings / font-feature-settings `normal | [<string> <number|integer>?]#` (string exactly 4 ASCII chars for feature tags; serialise `"wght" 700`); font-variant-alternates `normal | [stylistic(<ident>) || historical-forms || styleset(<ident>#) || character-variant(<ident>#) || swash(<ident>) || ornaments(<ident>) || annotation(<ident>)]`; font-variant as a real shorthand split over caps/numeric/ligatures/east-asian/alternates/position/emoji with `normal|none` exclusive (CSS Fonts 4 §6.1); font-synthesis split `none | [weight || style || small-caps || position]` into the four longhands and a fold back (computed of initial is `weight style small-caps position`); font-family per §2.1 (unquoted idents cannot start with a number, generic families lower-cased, `""` invalid). font-size-adjust `from-font` [..]

### @font-face descriptor grammars (src tech()/format()/local(), font-width, metric overrides) and the @font-palette-values rule are not modelled

- **134 subtests / 10 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: The @font-face src list is stored without validating its modifiers: `url("foo.ttf") tech()` and `format()` with no argument, `local(A) dummy()`, `tech(color-COLRv0)` must be lower-cased/keyword-checked (rules.cpp/source.cpp keep the author's text), the font-width descriptor (`auto | <font-width-absolute>{1,2}`) is refused, and @font-palette-values is parsed as a generic CSSRule with no CSSFontPaletteValuesRule interface (name, fontFamily, basePalette, overrideColors), so `rule.type`/`constructor.name` and every descriptor assertion fail; invalid @font-palette-values blocks are also kept instead of dropped (expected 25 rules, got 28).
- Spec: CSS Fonts 4 §4.3 (src descriptor, format(), tech()), §4.6 font-width descriptor, §4.10 metric overrides; CSS Fonts 4 §10.3 @font-palette-values and §10.4 CSSFontPaletteValuesRule
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/stylesheets/rules.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/stylesheets/source.cpp (font-face descriptors ~340-360); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/stylesheets/prototypes.cpp (font-width descriptor mention); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/parser.cpp (at-rule descriptors); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/urange.cpp (the one descriptor with a grammar today - the pattern to copy)
- Evidence: css/css-fonts/parsing/font-palette-values-invalid.html 26/27 (`parsing @font-palette-values :: expected 25 but got 28`), font-palette-values-valid 24/36 (`expected "CSSFontPaletteValuesRule" but got "CSSRule"`), font-palette-invalid 4; css/css-fonts/parsing/font-face-src-tech.html 25/39 (`src: url("foo.ttf") tech() is invalid :: expected false but got true`, `tech(color-COLRv0) :: expected [..]
- Fix: Give @font-face's src a grammar in source.cpp beside unicode-range: `[ <url> [format(<font-format>)]? [tech(<font-tech>#)]? | local(<family-name>) ]#`, dropping an item whose modifiers are malformed (the list keeps the valid items, CSS Fonts 4 §4.3), lower-casing format/tech keywords, refusing unknown function names in the item; add font-width/font-stretch (`auto | <percentage>{1,2} | keyword{1,2}`), ascent-override/descent-override/line-gap-override (`normal | <percentage>`), size-adjust. Add @font-palette-values to the parser's at-rule table with its four descriptors (font-family, base-palette `light|dark|<integer>`, override-colors `[<integer> <color>]#`) and a CSSFontPaletteValuesRule prototype (type 0 in CSSRule constants but a distinct constructor name) in prototypes.cpp; drop a block whose prelude is not a <dashed-ident>.

### Property ranges only clamp at zero: widows/orphans/column-count need >=1, font-size-adjust and font-stretch have no range at all

- **118 subtests / 7 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: numeric_text (easing.cpp:257-279) floors at 0 only when the table row says `nonnegative` and, for font-weight, hard-codes [1,1000] with a ponytail note 'give the table a range when a second one animates'. font-size-adjust (table.cpp:225) and font-stretch (216) are freeform rows without the flag, so an extrapolated `-1` / `-100%` is written; widows/orphans (298-299) and column-count (table_modules.cpp:97) are `<integer [1,∞]>` but only the zero floor applies, so `at (-3)` gives 0 where 1 is expected.
- Spec: CSS Break 3 §3.3 (orphans/widows: <integer [1,∞]>); CSS Multicol 1 §3.2 (column-count <integer [1,∞]>); CSS Fonts 4 §2.4 (font-width <percentage [0,∞]>), §2.6 (font-size-adjust <number [0,∞]>); CSS [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/easing.cpp (numeric_text 257-279); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table.cpp (216, 225, 298-299); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table_modules.cpp (97); ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/style/css/properties.hpp (property_syntax 73-93)
- Evidence: css/css-fonts/animations/font-size-adjust-interpolation.html 40 (`from neutral to [2] at (-2) :: expected "0" but got "- 1"`) and -composition (`add [0.0] to add [2.0] at (-0.5) :: expected "0" but got "- 1"`); css/css-break/animation/widows-interpolation.html 34 (`from [10] to [20] at (-3) :: expected "1" but got "0"`), orphans-interpolation 10; [..]
- Fix: Replace the bool with a `float minimum` (default -inf; 0 for today's nonnegative rows; 1 for widows, orphans, column-count, font-weight's [1,1000] as min/max) in property_syntax and clamp in numeric_text after rounding; retype font-size-adjust as number (keywords `none`, plus the `<font-metric>? <number>` two-token form handled in the font grammars cluster) and font-stretch as percentage, both minimum 0. Keep the `!out.has_percent` guard for lengths with an unresolved percentage.

### The engine's hand-kept inherits() list (25 names) gates inheritance and `unset`, so every other inherited property neither inherits nor resolves `unset` to the parent

- **114 subtests / 17 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: engine.hpp:353-374 `inherits()` is a fixed list (border-collapse ... writing-mode plus the three white-space longhands) that disagrees with the table's `inherited` flag (properties.hpp:80). It is consulted for `unset`/`revert` (engine.cpp:1352-1361: the declaration is erased and, when !inherits, the initial shows) and for building the inherited half (engine.cpp:2137-2143), so font-kerning, font-variant-*, font-feature/variation-settings, hyphens, line-break, overflow-wrap, word-break, tab-size, text-align-last, text-justify, text-orientation, text-combine-upright, orphans, widows, list-style-image, dominant-baseline, block-ellipsis... never reach a child, and `unset`/`inherit` on them in a [..]
- Spec: CSS Cascade 4 §7.1-7.3 (inherit, unset: 'if the property is inherited... acts as inherit'); each property's 'Inherited: yes' line (CSS Fonts 4, CSS Text 4, CSS Break 3, CSS Writing Modes 4)
- Engine files: ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/style/engine.hpp (inherits 353-374); ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp (1350-1361, 2137-2143); ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table.cpp and table_modules.cpp (the `inherited` column)
- Evidence: css/css-fonts/inheritance.html 16 (`Property font-kerning inherits :: expected "none" but got "auto"`, font-variant-caps expected small-caps got normal, font-feature-settings, font-variation-settings, font-size-adjust, font-stretch); css/css-text/inheritance.html 15 (`hyphens inherits :: expected "none" but got "manual"`, line-break, overflow-wrap, tab-size, text-align-last, word-break, [..]
- Fix: Make inherits() answer from the table: `if (const auto * p = css::find_property(property)) return p->inherited;` after the `--` branch, keeping the white-space longhand note only if their rows lack the flag (they have it: table_modules.cpp:228-233). Audit the table column against the specs for the 25 names (font-size stays inherited as px, the pre-pass already guarantees that). The cost is a larger inherited half per element for pages that set these; measure bench_style before and after (a shared inherited block is the existing mitigation). Then the `unset`-in-transition and `inherit`-keyframe cases behave without further work in css.cpp.

### border-spacing computes to one length instead of the pair, so its interpolation and serialisation are one-valued

- **72 subtests / 1 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: table.cpp:175 `{"border-spacing", k::freeform, "", "0px", true, false}`: the cascade keeps `10px` as written where CSS 2 defines the computed value as two absolute lengths (`10px 10px`), so getComputedStyle reads `7px` where `7px 7px` is expected and easing.cpp interpolates a scalar against a pair only by accident.

- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/properties/table.cpp (175); ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp (pass two length fold 2060-2100); ctbrowser/lib/Style/easing.cpp (list-of-numerics shape, numeric_of)

- Fix: A tiny grammar `<length [0,∞]>{1,2}` (refuse negatives and a third value) that canonicalises one value to `X X` at declaration time, so computed value and animation shape are always two lengths; interpolate as a two-numeric list (the padded-list path shadows/filters use, or two numeric_of calls) and serialise `a b`. Also drop the second value when equal only in el.style serialisation (CSSOM shortest form), not in the computed value.

Add table rows: font-synthesis (+ -weight/-style/-small-caps/-position, inherited, `none | weight || style || small-caps || position`), font-kerning (auto normal none, inherited), font-language-override (normal | <string>, inherited), font-feature-settings (normal | <string> <integer>?#, inherited, double-quoted serialisation), font-variant as a shorthand whose `normal`/`none` cannot combine. Then `font`'s computed read-back (already written in 7a4c7feb) has canonical longhands to print.

### :has() matching goes stale or misses after DOM mutation and form-state changes

- **251 subtests / 20 files**, size M, overlaps FLAT, confidence low
- Root cause: engine.cpp:1147-1173 answers `:has()` by running each argument as a scoped query on a second engine (`has_walker_`, built once and given `states_source_` at construction) anchored at the subject, or at the subject's parent when the argument's last combinator is a sibling one. The walker's root-bounded traversal (engine.cpp:705-716: 'ONLY THE PATH TO THE ROOT IS WALKED ABOVE IT') visits earlier siblings only at levels on the path, so an argument whose inner `:is()`/`:not()` reaches sideways or upward from a descendant (`#descendant:is(.p + .c_has_scope ~ .d .e)`) or that depends on siblings inserted/removed OUTSIDE the anchor's subtree answers false; the failing rows are exactly the [..]
- Spec: Selectors 4 §4.5 (:has() relative selectors, :scope anchoring), §3.4 (:is/:where forgiving), CSS Scoping 1 (host); HTML §4.16.2 pseudo-class state changes
- Engine files: /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/engine.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/selector.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/document/as_node.cpp

- Fix: Two candidate fixes; measure first. (1) Anchor correctly: for a `:has()` argument, run the relative selector with the SUBJECT as `:scope` but let the walker's matching of inner complex selectors (`:is(.p + .c_has_scope ~ .d .e)`) walk the real ancestors/siblings of the candidate, i.e. seed levels_ for the whole path to the document root rather than to `from`, and for `~`/`+` leading combinators start from the parent's first child. (2) Make the walker share the primary engine's per-node facts/state cache rather than its own, and reset it on every mutation generation (as_node.cpp:197 'mutated() makes every one of those a restyle' - confirm the walker is invalidated too). For has-complexity, cache per (subject, argument) answers per style pass. Form-state pseudo-classes: ensure the shell recomputes :checked/:indeterminate/:valid/:placeholder-shown/:default/:required/:out-of-range bits on [..]

- Spec: Selectors 4 §3.6 (pseudo-elements in compounds, what may follow), §4.5/§4.3 (:is/:has may not contain pseudo-elements); CSS Pseudo 4 §3 (tree-abiding), §2 (highlight pseudo-elements take no further [..]
- Engine files: /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/selector.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/stylesheets/serialize.cpp; /home/alex/Downloads/claude/wt/ctbrowser-wpt/ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/style/selector.hpp

- Fix: After a pseudo-element, set a `after_pseudo_element` flag on the compound: allow only user-action pseudo-classes (:hover :active :focus :focus-visible :focus-within, and :is()/:where() whose arguments are only those) for tree-abiding pseudo-elements and ::part(); for ::slotted() and highlight pseudo-elements allow nothing; any other simple selector, pseudo-element or combinator (except a second pseudo-element where CSS Pseudo 4 §3 allows `::before::marker`) sets invalid_. - Evidence: custom-elements/custom-element-reaction-queue.html 5/6 ('Upgrading … must not invoke attributeChangedCallback for the attribute changed during upgrading: expected 2 but got 5'); custom-elements/enqueue-custom-element-callback-reactions-inside-another-callback.html 4/8 (order 'begin','connected','end'); custom-elements/Document-createElementNS-prefix-timing.html 3, [..]

Keep run_upgrade's element queue but enqueue attributeChanged for the element's attributes and connected (if connected) BEFORE calling the constructor (HTML 4.13.5 steps 3-4), and mark reactions enqueued by the constructor for the same element as belonging to the same queue. ### XHR/fetch of a sibling file fails: the resolved file:// URL is treated as network

- **28 subtests / 7 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: ctdrive sets the page location to a `file://` URL (tools/ctdrive/ctdrive.cpp:455-473). `XMLHttpRequest.open` resolves a relative URL against it (ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/xhr.cpp:189-192: `resolve(location_href_, given)`), so `resources/empty-html-document.html` becomes `file:///…/custom-elements/resources/empty-html-document.html`. `dom_bindings::load_resource` (resources.cpp:375-400) only takes the page-relative asset branch when `url.find("://") == npos`; a `file://` URL falls to the network branch, `network_allowed_` is false in the runner (docs/wpt.md: `CTBROWSER_NETWORK=0`), so failure = 'network access is off …', status 0, and xhr.cpp:150 fires `error` → the helper rejects 'Failed [..]
- Spec: XHR §4.5.2 open step 8 (parse URL relative to the settings object's base URL), §4.5.6 send (network error → error event); HTML 7.2 origin of file: documents is the runner's business
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/resources.cpp:375-400 load_resource; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/xhr.cpp:188-192 open (URL resolution); ctbrowser/tools/ctdrive/ctdrive.cpp:455-473 (the file:// location)
- Evidence: custom-elements/adopted-callback.html: 10 'an HTML document fetched by XHR' subtests (promise_test: Unhandled rejection with value: "Failed to fetch the document"); custom-elements/connected-callbacks.html 5, custom-elements/disconnected-callbacks.html 5, custom-elements/upgrading.html 3, custom-elements/Document-createElement.html 3, custom-elements/connected-callbacks-html-fragment-parsing.html [..]
- Fix: In load_resource, before the network branch: if the URL's scheme is `file:` (or it is under the directory of location_href_), strip the scheme and read it through the same path as the relative branch (`assets_->load(path)`); status 200 with mime_for_path. One `if` in the funnel fixes XHR, fetch and `<link>`/`<script src>` alike. Keep the 404 → `load` (not `error`) behaviour.

In emit_shadow write: shadowrootmode, shadowrootdelegatesfocus, shadowrootserializable, shadowrootslotassignment="manual" when manual_slots, shadowrootclonable, shadowrootreferencetarget="…" when set, shadowrootcustomelementregistry when the tree carries a scoped registry. ### NewTarget is invisible to the native HTMLElement constructor: Reflect.construct / proxy constructors get 'Illegal constructor', .prototype is read too early and too often

- **26 subtests / 4 files**, size M, overlaps CE2, confidence high
- Root cause: custom_elements.cpp:26-30 (header 'WHAT A NATIVE CANNOT SEE: new.target'): `construct_html_element` (254) identifies the definition by the RECEIVER's prototype, because the VM carries new.target only into JS frames (include/ctbrowser/script/vm.hpp:971-974 construct(new_target), pending_new_target_ 1383). So `Reflect.construct(HTMLElement, [], NewTarget)` and a Proxy over the class throw 'Illegal constructor', and Get(NewTarget,'prototype') happens before the registry/extends checks and more than once (HTMLElement-constructor 'must only get .prototype once'). CE2's brief reports this as needing a `new_target()` accessor in lib/Script and not theirs.
- Spec: HTML 4.13.4 'HTML element constructors' steps 1-8 (registry lookup by NewTarget, Get(NewTarget,'prototype') once, fallback to realm's HTMLElement.prototype); ECMA-262 10.1.14 [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/script/vm.hpp:971-974, 1383-1392 (pending_new_target_ / pass_new_target), ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/ (expose new.target to a native frame); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/custom_elements.cpp:254-345 construct_html_element
- Evidence: custom-elements/htmlconstructor/newtarget.html 10/10 ('Use NewTarget's prototype, not the one stored at definition time': TypeError: Illegal constructor); custom-elements/htmlconstructor/newtarget-customized-builtins.html 10/10; custom-elements/HTMLElement-constructor.html 4/12 ('must only get .prototype once'), custom-elements/HTMLElement-constructor-customized-builtins.html 2/2 ('must not get [..]
- Fix: Add `value context::new_target() const` returning the pending new.target for the innermost native constructor frame (construct() already receives it; keep it in the frame record). In construct_html_element: NewTarget = new_target() (undefined/the callee for plain `new`); if NewTarget === the active function → TypeError; look up the definition whose constructor === NewTarget in the registry (HTML 4.13.4 step 2-3); THEN Get(NewTarget,'prototype') exactly once; if not an object use NewTarget's realm's HTMLElement.prototype (single realm today); create/upgrade the element with that prototype. Remove the receiver-prototype heuristic. JS/VM semantics line: no bytecode change - only a native-frame accessor.

Add 'filter' to the -webkit- alias list so webkitFilter/-webkit-filter map to filter. commitStyles: write the animation's current computed values of the animated properties into the style attribute (one setAttribute → the reaction follows for free).

### Event path: relatedTarget never stops the path, non-composed events stop at every shadow root, closed trees are hidden from composedPath only when the target is inside one

- **20 subtests / 6 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: Three missing steps of DOM 2.9 'dispatch'. (1) Step 5.9.7 'if parent is relatedTarget (after retargeting against parent) set parent to null' is not implemented: dispatch.cpp:316-375 retargets relatedTarget per step but never truncates the path, so an event at B1a with relatedTarget B1 walks on to A (event-with-related-target: expected 5 steps got 9; event-composed-path-with-related-target: expected 2 got 4). (2) `propagation_path` (events/input.cpp:372-420) makes a shadow root's parent null for EVERY shadow root when !composed; the spec's 'get the parent' for a ShadowRoot returns null only when the root is the root of the ORIGINAL target's invocation target, so a non-composed event at a [..]
- Spec: DOM 2.9 'dispatch' steps 5.3-5.9 (5.9.1 slot-in-closed-tree, 5.9.7 'Otherwise, if parent is relatedTarget, then set parent to null'), 'get the parent' for ShadowRoot (4.2.2), 2.10 composedPath() [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/dispatch.cpp:272-380 (retarget, relatedTarget handling), 425-485 (closed_to / per-step composedPath); ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/events/input.cpp:372-420 propagation_path
- Evidence: shadow-dom/event-with-related-target.html 6/18 ('The event path must be correct' lengths differ 5 vs 9); shadow-dom/event-composed-path-with-related-target.html 6/13 (expected 2 but got 4; expected 17 but got 19); shadow-dom/Extensions-to-Event-Interface.html 4/16 (scoped + relatedTarget through slot; composed with relatedTarget A2-S); shadow-dom/event-composed-path.html 2/11 (closed slots [..]
- Fix: Build the path in dispatch as spec 5.9 does, in one loop that owns the three rules: keep `original_root = root_of_tree(target)`; at each `parent = get_the_parent(node)` (assigned slot, else parent, else for a shadow root: host if composed OR root != original_root, else null); compute `rt = retarget(relatedTarget, parent)`; if parent is a node and `rt == parent` stop; record per step {node, shadow_adjusted_target, related=rt, root_of_closed_tree, slot_in_closed_tree} as the struct the spec appends. composedPath() per listener then filters by the two closed-tree flags exactly as DOM 2.10 (currentTargetHiddenSubtreeLevel walk) with no target_in_shadow gate. propagation_path's document/window tail stays. The retarget lambda (dispatch.cpp:293) becomes a dom_bindings member so cluster 5 can use it.

### `:state()` is parsed as a functional pseudo-class but never matched, serialised with spaces, and CustomStateSet changes do not restyle

- **17 subtests / 5 files**, size S, overlaps CE2, confidence high
- Root cause: lib/Style/css/selector.cpp:150 lists 'state' among the functional pseudo-class names (so `:state(foo)` parses) but nothing matches it - the only element state the bindings hand the style engine is the bit set (`structural_defined` selector.cpp:57, include/ctbrowser/style/selector.hpp:152; `:defined` is the pattern). The CustomStateSet (element_internals.cpp:514-560) lives on the internals object only, so add/delete/clear neither reach the matcher nor invalidate style. Serialisation of the argument keeps the raw token whitespace (':state( \(escaped\ state )').
- Spec: HTML 4.13.8 CustomStateSet + `:state()` pseudo-class ('matches when the element's custom state set contains the ident'), CSS Selectors 4 §17 custom state pseudo-class serialisation, Selectors 4 §3.9 [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Style/css/selector.cpp:57,150,1116 (structural_defined pattern; functional name list; serialiser); ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/style/selector.hpp:152; ctbrowser/lib/Shell/bindings/element_internals.cpp:514-560 (CustomStateSet); the pseudo-state feed from the bindings to lib/Style (where :defined is set)
- Evidence: custom-elements/state/state-pseudo-class.html 6/8 (':state(' must throw; ':state(foo) serialization'; ':state(foo) in simple cases'); custom-elements/state/state-css-selector.html 5/10, state-css-selector-shadow-dom.html 3/3, state-css-selector-nth-of.html 2/3 (expected rgb(0,255,0) got rgb(255,0,0)); custom-elements/state/ElementInternals-states.html 1 (iteration while mutating)
- Fix: Keep the states on the node side of the feed (a per-node small vector<string> beside the pseudo-state bits, set by CustomStateSet add/delete/clear which also call the restyle the bits use), match `state(<ident>)` by membership, reject `:state(` / non-ident arguments at parse time (SyntaxError from querySelector), serialise as `:state(` + escaped ident + `)`. The CE2 agent owns `:defined` in the same file; this is the same shape - hand it to them or land it first.

### Slots outside a shadow tree and flatten semantics: fallback children counted, nested light-DOM slots recursed, slot.assign duplicates kept

- **7 subtests / 5 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: `assignedNodes({flatten:true})` (shadow_dom.cpp:350-378) recurses into ANY <slot> among the assigned nodes and substitutes the slot's own children when the list is empty, without checking that the slot's root is a shadow root; DOM 4.2.2.3 'find flattened slottables' returns empty for a slot whose root is not a shadow root and only recurses into a slottable slot whose root IS a shadow root. `slot.assign` (385-430) stores the arguments as given, so `assign(c1, c1, c2, c2, c1)` yields three entries instead of an ordered set.
- Spec: DOM 4.2.2.2 'find slottables' step 1-2, 4.2.2.3 'find flattened slottables' steps 1 and 4.2, HTMLSlotElement.assign ('set this's manually assigned nodes to the given nodes' as an ordered set)

- Evidence: shadow-dom/slots.html 'Distributed nodes for Slots not in a shadow tree' (expected [] got [c1]), shadow-dom/slots-fallback.html 'Remove a slot which is a fallback content of another slot', shadow-dom/slots-fallback-in-document.html 1, shadow-dom/slots-outside-shadow-dom.html 'Light DOM slot element should be in flattened assignedNodes'; shadow-dom/imperative-slot-api.html 3 ('first one wins'; [..]
- Fix: In the flatten gather: return [] when the slot's root is not a shadow root; recurse into a nested slot only when its own root is a shadow root, else append it as a node. In assign: dedupe preserving first occurrence. For the cross-tree cases keep the manual list as given and let assigned_nodes_of filter by `parent == host` at read time (it already does) - check the two remaining assertions against DOM 4.2.2.1 step 2 after the dedupe.

## t262-builtins — test262 test/built-ins (all directories); intl402 is in the corpus (~/.cache/ctbrowser/test262/test/intl402, 41 dirs) but has NO result JSON at 9f8da347 - it was never run, so it is unmeasured rather than failing

Baseline: /tmp/m-9f8da347/test-built-ins.json: 23,812 files; 16,848 PASS, 6,190 FAIL, 1 TIMEOUT, 3 CRASH, 770 SKIP (73.1% of the 23,042 that ran). test262 has one result per file (no subtests), so failing_subtests == failing_files below. 4,603 of the 6,194 non-passes are Temporal (ReferenceError: Temporal is not defined), 927 RegExp, 170 Array, 77 Object, 61 ShadowRealm, 57 Proxy, 56 [..]

### Temporal does not exist

- **4611 subtests / 4611 files**, size L, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: There is no `Temporal` global at all - `rg Temporal ctbrowser/lib/Script` finds nothing - so every file under built-ins/Temporal fails at its first line (4,113 'ReferenceError: Temporal is not defined at <script>', 119 'Calling as constructor Expected a TypeError but got a ReferenceError', the rest inside helpers) and Date.prototype.toTemporalInstant (8) is undefined. This is one missing proposal, not an engine defect, and it is 74% of the slice's FAIL column.
- Spec: tc39/proposal-temporal (ES2026 draft §21.x Temporal), ISO 8601 / RFC 9557 (IXDTF) string grammar
- Engine files: ctbrowser/tools/ct262/ct262.cpp (the feature skip list at lines 170-235: Atomics/SharedArrayBuffer/cross-realm are skipped by frontmatter `features:`; Temporal is not); ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/values.cpp (where a Temporal namespace would be installed beside Date); ctbrowser/docs/test262.md
- Evidence: test/built-ins/Temporal/** - 4,603 files, all FAIL, cause 'runtime: uncaught ReferenceError: Temporal is not defined' (4,151) or a helper-level ReferenceError; test/built-ins/Date/prototype/toTemporalInstant/*.js - 8 files ('`toTemporalInstant` is undefined, not a function')
- Fix: Two honest options. (S, recommended first) add `Temporal` to ct262's feature skip list next to Atomics, with the reason written in docs/test262.md ('skipped, by reason: Temporal: not implemented'), so the FAIL column measures the engine again - the 4,611 become SKIP, not PASS. (L, a whole session or more) implement the proposal: Temporal.Instant/PlainDate/PlainTime/PlainDateTime/PlainYearMonth/PlainMonthDay/ZonedDateTime/Duration/Now, the ISO 8601 / RFC 9557 string grammar (the tests' 'reject minus zero as extended year', 'no more than N decimal places', 'annotation keys must be lowercase' rows are that grammar), the iso8601 calendar only, UTC and fixed-offset time zones only (no tzdata), and `Temporal.Duration` arithmetic with the balancing rules. Nothing in the browser corpus needs it.

### ShadowRealm (and $262.createRealm) - a second realm in one context

- **64 subtests / 64 files**, size L, overlaps REALM, confidence high
- Root cause: `ShadowRealm` is not defined (58 'ReferenceError: ShadowRealm is not defined'; 2 'This test must fail if ShadowRealm...'). ct262.cpp:216-231 refuses `$262.createRealm` because 'a realm here is a script::context, which owns its heap' - the same single-realm fact the REALM brief addresses (a realm = a globals table + a global object inside ONE context, so values do not cross heaps). ShadowRealm is that machinery plus WrappedFunctionCreate. The 162 `cross-realm` SKIPs and TypedArrayConstructors/internals/DefineOwnProperty/*detached-buffer-throws-realm (2) + Proxy/revocable/tco-fn-realm (1) are the same gap.
- Spec: tc39/proposal-shadowrealm §3 (ShadowRealm constructor, 3.2 WrappedFunctionCreate, GetWrappedValue); ES §9.3 Realms
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/objects/context.cpp; ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/call/invoke.cpp (context::call switching realm); ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/values.cpp (the ShadowRealm constructor next to the other globals); ctbrowser/tools/ct262/ct262.cpp:216-231 ($262.createRealm)
- Evidence: test/built-ins/ShadowRealm/** - 61 of 64 FAIL, 58 'ShadowRealm is not defined'; test/built-ins/TypedArrayConstructors/internals/DefineOwnProperty/BigInt/detached-buffer-throws-realm.js and ../DefineOwnProperty/detached-buffer-throws-realm.js - '$262.createRealm: this host has one realm'; test/built-ins/Proxy/revocable/tco-fn-realm.js - same message; 162 files SKIPped by the `cross-realm` feature [..]
- Fix: After REALM lands: `new ShadowRealm()` = allocate a fresh realm record (globals table + global object + fresh intrinsics) in the SAME context; `evaluate(src)` compiles a classic script and runs it with that realm current, returning primitives as-is and callables through WrappedFunctionCreate (3.2: a native whose [[Call]] switches realm, passes primitives/wrapped callables, throws TypeError for objects); `importValue` = the module loader against the shadow realm's module map. Then `$262.createRealm` returns `{global, evalScript}` for the new realm and the cross-realm skip goes away.

### Proxy [[Call]] is missing and the descriptor/extensibility trap invariants are not checked

- **53 subtests / 53 files**, size M, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: (a) [[Call]] on a callable proxy: `context::invoke` (vm/call/invoke.cpp, the function head handles only heap_kind::native and heap_kind::function) and op::call in run_loop.cpp have no proxy branch - only `construct` does (run_loop.cpp:1105-1117, construct.cpp:132-152) - so `p(...)`/`p.call(...)` either errors '`sumProxy` is function, not a function' or reaches the target without ever reading the `apply` trap (no string "apply" appears anywhere under lib/Script/vm). (b) descriptors.cpp:150-185 says it outright: 'The invariant checks against the target (steps 15-22) are not made' for getOwnPropertyDescriptor; defineProperty (descriptors.cpp:490-500) returns the trap's truthiness without steps [..]
- Spec: ES2025 §10.5.1-10.5.13 (Proxy internal methods), §10.5.12 [[Call]], §10.5.5 [[GetOwnProperty]] steps 11-22, §10.5.6 [[DefineOwnProperty]] steps 10-23, §10.5.10 [[Delete]] steps 10-14, §10.5.3-10.5.4 [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/call/invoke.cpp (context::invoke, context::proxy_trap at :297); ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/run_loop.cpp:1105-1117 (op::call needs the same proxy hand-off op::construct has); ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/objects/descriptors.cpp:150-185, :393-405, :490-500; ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/objects/store.cpp:170-200; ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/objects/chain.cpp (is_extensible / prototype_of for [..]
- Evidence: test/built-ins/Proxy/apply/*.js (9) + create-target-is-not-a-constructor.js + create-target-is-revoked-function-proxy.js + revocable/target-is-revoked-function-proxy.js (typeof must stay 'function') = 12 files for [[Call]]; test/built-ins/Proxy/getOwnPropertyDescriptor/*.js (8) - 'Expected a TypeError to be thrown but no exception was thrown at all'; test/built-ins/Proxy/defineProperty/*.js (8), [..]
- Fix: [[Call]]: in `invoke`, `if (callable.is_kind(heap_kind::proxy))` -> proxy_trap("apply"); if absent, `invoke(p->target, args, this_value)`; else `call(trap, {target, this_value, CreateArrayFromList(args)}, handler)` (10.5.12). Make op::call take the same 'long way round' branch op::construct already has. `value::is_callable` for a proxy already answers from the target (value.hpp:300). Invariants: write one `validate_proxy_gopd(target_desc, trap_desc, extensible)` per 10.5.5 steps 11-22 (undefined answer refused when target key is non-configurable or target non-extensible; IsCompatiblePropertyDescriptor; non-configurable answer only when target's is; non-writable+non-configurable only if target's is) and the mirror for [[DefineOwnProperty]] 10.5.6 steps 10-23 and [[Delete]] 10.5.10 steps 10-14 (target key non-configurable, or target non-extensible -> TypeError). Route [..]

### Array element reads never leave the array: no prototype-chain fallthrough past items.size(), and non-index numeric keys are dropped

- **27 subtests / 27 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: `context::lookup_index` (vm/objects/lookup.cpp:122-165) is the whole [[Get]] for a number key on an array. A hole INSIDE `items` (element_attrs elem_hole) does walk Array.prototype and from_object_prototype (:141-149), but an index at or beyond `items.size()` (after `x.length = N` grew the length without allocating, or simply past the end) returns undefined at :162 without consulting Array.prototype[N] or a subclass prototype - so every S15.4.4.x_A4 test (`Array.prototype[1] = -1; x = [0]; x.length = 2; x[1]`) reads undefined. The same fast path treats EVERY number key as an element: 4294967295 (not an array index, 10.4.2 says it is an ordinary property), -1, 1.5, NaN and Infinity fall to [..]
- Spec: ES2025 §10.4.2 Array exotic objects (an array index is 0..2^32-2), §10.1.8 OrdinaryGet steps 2-3 (undefined own -> parent.[[Get]]), §10.1.9 OrdinarySet, §7.1.21
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/objects/lookup.cpp:122-165 (lookup_index); ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/objects/store.cpp:1-70 (store_index); ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/objects/chain.cpp (has_property for an index past items.size()); ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/internal.hpp:286-288 element_at, :369-380 has_element
- Evidence: 15 files whose cause contains 'Array.prototype[N] =': Array/prototype/{join/S15.4.4.5_A5_T1, pop/S15.4.4.6_A4_T1-2, reverse/S15.4.4.8_A4_T1-2, shift/S15.4.4.9_A4_T1-2, slice/S15.4.4.10_A4_T1, sort/S15.4.4.11_A6_T2, splice/S15.4.4.12_A4_T1-3, toLocaleString/S15.4.4.3_A3_T1, toString/S15.4.4.2_A3_T1, unshift/S15.4.4.13_A4_T2}.js; Object/defineProperty/15.2.3.6-4-184.js,-185,-186 and [..]
- Fix: In lookup_index: when the key is a number, first `if (!is_array_index(d)) return lookup_property(target, number_to_string(d));` (covers 2^32-1, negatives, fractions, NaN, Infinity); then for an array whose `i >= items.size()` and not in `sparse`, fall through to the prototype walk the hole branch already does (factor lines 141-149 into `hole_read(target, i)` and call it from both places). Same two guards in store_index (a non-index number key -> store_property(ToString(key)); an out-of-range index still checks the chain for a setter/non-writable inherited property per OrdinarySet before growing). has_element/has_property need the chain walk for an index past items.size() too (the -2-17 arguments tests and S15.4.5.1_A1.2_T3 read `hasOwnProperty` vs `in`).

### JS strings are UTF-8 bytes, not UTF-16 code units (the architectural string cluster)

- **27 subtests / 27 files**, size L, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: docs/script.md 'The UTF-16 gap' states it: string_object::text is UTF-8, `length`/`charCodeAt`/`at`/indices are byte positions, `String.fromCharCode(0xFFFF)` is three bytes whose first byte (239) is what charCodeAt returns, a lone surrogate cannot be represented at all (padStart/padEnd/at/isWellFormed/toWellFormed/StringIterator all expect "\uD83D" to survive), `codePointAt` equals charCodeAt, RegExp `.` without `u` must match ONE code unit of a supplementary character and match indices / lastIndex are code-unit offsets ('The length of "𝐁" is 2 Expected «4», «2»'). Every one of these is the representation, not a method bug; the regex engine (regex.hpp) matches UTF-8 runs and decodes code [..]
- Spec: ES2025 §6.1.4 The String Type (sequence of UTF-16 code units), §22.1.3 String.prototype methods (all index by code unit), §22.2.2.7.3 Canonicalize / §22.2.4 RegExp pattern semantics (code unit vs [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/script/value.hpp (string_object); ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/text/string.cpp (every index-taking method: 849 lines); ctbrowser/include/ctbrowser/script/regex.hpp + ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/text/regexp.cpp (positions, `.`, classes, lastIndex, indices); ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/objects/lookup.cpp:166-170 (string index), ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/coerce.cpp [..]
- Evidence: String/length.js ('Unicode escape (surrogate pair) Expected «4», «2»'), String/prototype/at/returns-code-unit.js, String/prototype/isWellFormed/returns-boolean.js, String/prototype/toWellFormed/returns-well-formed-string.js, String/prototype/padStart/normal-operation.js, String/prototype/padEnd/normal-operation.js, StringIteratorPrototype/next/next-iteration-surrogate-pairs.js (7); [..]
- Fix: Change string_object::text to a UTF-16 representation (std::u16string, or a Latin-1/UTF-16 two-mode string as V8/SM do so ASCII pages pay nothing), convert at the boundaries (compile/strings.cpp literals decode escapes to code units; DOM bindings and the Shell convert UTF-8<->UTF-16 once per crossing; JSON, to_string of numbers stay ASCII). Re-base every index-taking String method, the string iterator (code points over pairs), the regex engine's subject cursor (a 'character' is a code unit unless `u`/`v`, then a code point over a surrogate pair; classes and `\p` already work in code points), lastIndex/indices, `String.fromCharCode` (ToUint16), `normalize`/`isWellFormed`. Keep goldens: nothing rendered changes for well-formed text. The acceptance list is unittests/js/string_basics.cpp's pinned section. This is the honest size: L - a whole agent session, the largest architectural item [..]

### Intrinsic prototype objects have the wrong shape: Function.prototype is a table, Array.prototype is not an array, NativeError.prototype has [[ErrorData]]

- **24 subtests / 24 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence high
- Root cause: (a) %Function.prototype% is `cx.prototype(proto_kind::function)`, a plain object_object table (function.cpp:449-453: `->set("prototype", table)` - which also gives Function.prototype the default {writable, enumerable, configurable} attributes instead of {false,false,false}); it is not callable, has no own `length`/`name`, and Object.prototype.toString says '[object Object]'. (b) %Array.prototype% is likewise a table: `Array.isArray(Array.prototype)` is false, `Array.prototype.length` is undefined, and `"length" in Object.create(Array.prototype)` is false (which is what Proxy/has/trap-is-undefined*.js actually measure). (c) Object.prototype.toString tags 'Error' by `inherits_from(error [..]
- Spec: ES2025 §20.2.3 Function.prototype (a built-in function object, length 0, name ""), §20.2.2.2 Function.prototype attributes {false,false,false}, §23.1.3 Array.prototype is an Array exotic object, [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/objects/function.cpp:440-475; ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/collections/array.cpp (where Array.prototype is installed) and ctbrowser/lib/Script/vm/objects/context.cpp (proto_kind::array); ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/objects/object.cpp:300-375 (Object.prototype.toString); ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/objects/errors.cpp:55-90, :105-185
- Evidence: Function/prototype/S15.3.4_A1.js ('must return "[object Function]"'), S15.3.4_A2_T1.js, S15.3.4_A2_T2.js, S15.3.4_A2_T3.js ('`prototype` is object, not a function'), Function/prototype/length.js, name.js ('should be an own property'), property-order.js, Function/prototype/S15.3.3.1_A1.js, _A2, _A3 (Function.prototype descriptor) - 10 files; Array/isArray/15.4.3.2-0-5.js, [..]
- Fix: (a) Make %Function.prototype% a native_object whose fn returns undefined (`is_constructor = false`, length 0, name ""), on Object.prototype, and point proto_kind::function at it - every closure's [[Prototype]] walk already goes through the table lookup, so the kind change is local to context.cpp + function.cpp; define `Function.prototype` on the ctor with attr_none. (b) Make %Array.prototype% an array_object of length 0 whose prototype is Object.prototype (10.4.2 says it IS an Array exotic); the `prototype(proto_kind::array)` fast path in lookup.cpp:141-149 then reads its named table. (c) Tag 'Error' when `find(error_stack_slot)` is present instead of inherits_from; instances get the slot at errors.cpp:168 already. (d) stack accessor: install via method_native with arity, TypeError for non-object receivers.

### Live iterators and keyed collections snapshot instead of reading the collection each step

- **19 subtests / 19 files**, size S, overlaps none, confidence medium
- Root cause: Array iterators created on an empty array report done on the first next() even after a push ('First result `done` flag Expected «true», «false»'), so the iterator caches length/exhaustion at creation rather than re-reading `length` on every next (23.1.5.1: the iterator reads len from the array each step and sets [[ArrayLike]] to undefined only once exhausted); the keyed store compacts on delete rather than leaving an empty entry, so a Map/Set iterator or forEach in flight skips an element after `delete` and re-`add` revisits at the end instead of in place (24.1.3.5/24.2.3.4: entries are set to ~empty~, appended on re-add); Set.prototype.isSubsetOf/isDisjointFrom iterate a snapshot of `this` [..]
- Spec: ES2025 §23.1.5.1 CreateArrayIterator (reads length each step), §24.1.3.5 Map.prototype.delete / §24.1.5.1 CreateMapIterator (empty entries), §24.2.3.x Set methods and §24.2.4 SetRecord iteration, [..]
- Engine files: ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/collections/array_iteration.cpp (the array iterator's next); ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/collections/keyed.cpp:120-200 (set_record/each_key), the Map/Set entry store and its delete; ctbrowser/lib/Script/builtins/collections/iterator.cpp (Map/Set iterator next)
- Evidence: Array/prototype/entries/iteration-mutable.js, keys/iteration-mutable.js, values/iteration-mutable.js, ArrayIteratorPrototype/next/iteration-mutable.js (4); Array/prototype/{entries,keys,values}/resizable-buffer-shrink-mid-iteration.js (3), Array/prototype/{entries,keys}/return-abrupt-from-this.js (2), Array/prototype/values/this-val-non-obj-coercible.js; [..]
- Fix: Array iterator: store {array, index, kind}; each next re-reads LengthOfArrayLike (or the view's length with the out-of-bounds TypeError) and only sets array=undefined when index >= len. Keyed store: on delete write a tombstone (empty key marker) and keep the vector; the iterator holds an index into it and skips tombstones; `clear` tombstones every entry (so an in-flight iterator ends) and `add` of a deleted key appends. Compact only when no iterator is live (or never; tombstone count vs size). Set algebra: iterate `this`'s entry vector by index, re-checking the entry is not a tombstone on each step, rather than copying keys first.
